中文摘要翻译: 从参考视频中克隆相机运动是视频生成中的一项重要任务，因为视频提供了直观且精确的控制。现有方法要么直接使用参数化表示而无法处理多镜头生成，要么合成跨配对数据但面临数据稀缺问题，导致在复杂的相机运动克隆中表现不佳。为解决这些问题，我们引入了一种通用的相机运动表示，将相机编码为网格运动视频。这种相机网格以视觉方式表示相机参数，并支持集成多样化的轨迹以进行多镜头视频生成。在此基础上，我们提出了OmniDirector——一个在百万级相机网格-视频对上训练的统一框架，该框架协调角色、动作和相机，为多模态扩散变换器提供导演级的控制。此外，我们设计了一种新颖的层次化提示扩展代理，通过理解信号间关系，系统描述相机运动和视觉内容，从而和谐地融合不同的控制信号。大量实验证明了我们框架的优越性能和出色的可控性。项目页面：https://ymlinfeng.github.io/OmniDirector.github.io/

</details>


### [58] [VISA: VLM-Guided Instance Semantic Auditing for 3D Occupancy World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13460)
*Ruiqi Xian,Yuehan Xian,Jing Liang,Xuewei Qi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VISA方法，将VLM作为训练时的语义审计器，通过结构化审计和知识蒸馏改进3D占据模型的闭集mIoU，尤其在目标和稀有类别上效果显著。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有将VLM特征与裁剪-字幕嵌入对齐的策略能提升文本空间相似度，但无法可靠改善闭集占据模型的mIoU；需要一种新的方式让VLM真正服务于3D占据感知。

Method: VISA在训练时对每个物理对象实例的裁剪区域查询离线VLM，获取包含类别假设、可能混淆、可靠性、属性和证据的结构化审计结果，沿目标轨迹传播，将审计结果与匹配的3D体素对齐，通过可靠性加权的分类学损失、属性因子损失和场景级审计图损失蒸馏到语义logits中，推理时不依赖VLM。

Result: 在nuScenes上，VISA将OccWorld的mIoU从19.06提升至20.05，GaussianWorld从21.36提升至21.91；GaussianWorld的目标mIoU从18.18提升至19.16，稀有类mIoU从15.60提升至16.79。

Conclusion: VLM更适合作为可靠性感知的语义审计器而非通用字幕嵌入目标来提升闭集占据模型的性能。

Original Abstract: Semantic 3D occupancy provides a voxelized world state for autonomous driving and robot decision making, but object and rare-class errors can affect free-space interpretation, collision checking, and temporal state propagation. We show that a common VLM strategy, aligning 3D voxel or object features with crop-caption embeddings, improves text-space similarity without reliably improving closed-set occupancy mIoU. Motivated by this mismatch, we propose VISA, a training-time semantic auditing approach for existing occupancy world models. VISA queries an offline VLM on a representative crop of each physical object instance, obtains a structured audit with class hypotheses, plausible confusions, reliability, attributes, and evidence, and propagates it along the object track. The audit is grounded to matched 3D object voxels and distilled into semantic logits through reliability-weighted taxonomy, attribute-factor, and scene-level audit graph losses, while inference remains unchanged and requires no VLM. On nuScenes, averaged across three runs, VISA improves OccWorld from 19.06 to 20.05 mIoU and GaussianWorld from 21.36 to 21.91 mIoU; on GaussianWorld, object mIoU improves from 18.18 to 19.16 and rare-class mIoU from 15.60 to 16.79. These results suggest that VLMs are better suited to closed-set occupancy as reliability-aware semantic auditors than as generic caption-embedding targets.

中文摘要翻译: 语义3D占据表示为自动驾驶和机器人决策提供了体素化的世界状态，但物体和稀有类别的错误会影响自由空间解释、碰撞检测和时间状态传播。我们发现，一种常见的VLM策略——将3D体素或物体特征与裁剪-字幕嵌入对齐——能够提升文本空间相似度，但无法可靠改善闭集占据mIoU。受此不匹配启发，我们提出VISA，一种针对现有占据世界模型的训练时语义审计方法。VISA对每个物理对象实例的代表性裁剪区域查询离线VLM，获取包含类别假设、可能混淆、可靠性、属性和证据的结构化审计结果，并沿目标轨迹传播。该审计结果与匹配的3D物体体素对齐，并通过可靠性加权的分类学损失、属性因子损失和场景级审计图损失蒸馏到语义logits中，而推理过程保持不变且无需VLM。在nuScenes上，三次运行取平均，VISA将OccWorld的mIoU从19.06提升至20.05，GaussianWorld从21.36提升至21.91；在GaussianWorld上，目标mIoU从18.18提升至19.16，稀有类mIoU从15.60提升至16.79。这些结果表明，VLM作为可靠性感知的语义审计器比作为通用字幕嵌入目标更适合提升闭集占据模型的性能。

</details>


### [59] [Point-Wise Geometry-Aware Transformer for Partial-to-Full Point Cloud Registration in Computer-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13488)
*Siyu Zhou,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GAPR-Net，一种结合卷积与Transformer的粗到细点云配准框架，通过跨注意力机制融合局部与全局信息，并引入变换不变的点几何特征，在骨部分到整体配准中达到94.2%召回率、1.992 mm RMSE。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 部分到整体点云配准因重叠率变化、点密度波动和噪声而具有挑战性；现有Transformer方法仅用于全局上下文聚合，忽略了精细局部几何信息，难以实现精确对应。

Method: 提出GAPR-Net，采用粗到细架构，结合卷积与Transformer模块，通过跨注意力机制在部分与整体点云之间融合局部和全局信息；并提出一种变换不变的点级几何特征表示，鲁棒地捕捉每个点相对于邻域的相对几何特征。

Result: 在胫骨、股骨、骨盆和胸肋软骨四种几何结构不同的骨骼上进行实验，整体配准召回率达94.2%，RMSE低至1.992 mm，旋转和翻译的R²值分别为0.908和0.974。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了部分到整体点云配准问题，实现了高精度3D配准，为计算机辅助手术中的精确手术导航和机器人介入提供了关键基础。

Original Abstract: Partial-to-full registration remains challenging due to varying overlap ratios, fluctuating point densities, and the presence of noise. While transformers have shown strong potential for point cloud processing, prior methods typically confine them to global context aggregation, overlooking fine-grained local geometry crucial for accurate correspondence. We propose \emph{GAPR-Net}, a learning-based point cloud registration framework with a coarse-to-fine architecture that combines convolution and transformer modules, in which local and global information is fused between the partial and full point clouds using a cross-attention mechanism. To achieve this, a transformation-invariant point-wise geometric feature representation is proposed, which can robustly capture relative geometric features for individual points with respect to their neighboring points. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, experiments are conducted on four geometrically distinct bones, including the tibia, femur, pelvis, and thoracic cartilage. The overall registration recall reaches 94.2\%, the method results in a low RMSE of 1.992 mm and $R^2$ values of 0.908 and 0.974 for rotation and translation, respectively. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively addresses the partial-to-full point cloud registration problem. The proposed method enables highly accurate 3D point cloud registration using partial observation, providing a critical foundation for precise surgical navigation and robotic interventions in computer-assisted surgery. The code will be accessed after the double-blind review process.

中文摘要翻译: 部分到整体的配准由于重叠率变化、点密度波动以及噪声的存在而仍具挑战性。尽管Transformer在点云处理中展现出强大的潜力，但现有方法通常将其局限于全局上下文聚合，忽略了对于精确对应至关重要的细粒度局部几何信息。我们提出了GAPR-Net，一种基于学习的点云配准框架，采用粗到细的架构，结合了卷积与Transformer模块，并通过跨注意力机制在部分点云与整体点云之间融合局部和全局信息。为此，我们提出了一种变换不变的点级几何特征表示，该表示能够鲁棒地捕捉每个点相对于其邻域点的相对几何特征。为了评估所提方法的有效性，我们在四种几何结构不同的骨骼（包括胫骨、股骨、骨盆和胸肋软骨）上进行了实验。整体配准召回率达到94.2%，该方法取得了低至1.992 mm的RMSE，旋转和翻译的R²值分别为0.908和0.974。结果表明，所提方法有效解决了部分到整体点云配准问题。该方法利用部分观测实现了高精度3D点云配准，为计算机辅助手术中的精确手术导航和机器人介入提供了关键基础。代码将在双盲评审结束后开放获取。

</details>


### [60] [Budget-Constrained Step-Level Diffusion Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13496)
*Mingkun Lei,Tong Zhao,Liangyu Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BudCache提出了一种预算驱动的扩散模型缓存加速方法，通过离线搜索最优缓存策略而非在线阈值判断，在固定计算预算下最大化生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有步级缓存方法使用基于阈值的启发式规则逐步决定是否缓存，没有直接优化最终输出质量，且推理延迟随输入变化、难以在部署时控制。

Method: 提出BudCache，预先固定计算预算，结合模拟退火与确定性爬山算法离线搜索最优缓存策略；在预算极紧时引入缓存感知的调度对齐，调整时间离散化以减少缓存引起的轨迹失配。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的实验表明，在相同推理预算下，BudCache的生成质量优于基于启发式的缓存基线方法。

Conclusion: BudCache通过离线搜索策略替代在线阈值判断，实现了可控、高效的扩散模型缓存加速，在不引入在线开销的前提下显著提升生成质量。

Original Abstract: Step-level caching accelerates diffusion models by exploiting temporal redundancy across denoising steps. Existing methods make per-step cache decisions using threshold-based heuristics, without directly optimizing for final output quality. As a result, their inference latency varies across inputs and is difficult to control at deployment. In this work, we propose BudCache, which inverts this formulation: rather than letting per-step error thresholds dictate the runtime cost, we fix the compute budget in advance and search for the cache policy that best preserves the final output. To tackle the combinatorial complexity of step selection, we combine Simulated Annealing with deterministic Hill Climbing. This offline search identifies high-quality cache policies within minutes and introduces no online search or thresholding overhead during inference. When the compute budget is very tight, we further introduce cache-aware schedule alignment, which adapts the time discretization to the selected cache policy to reduce cache-induced trajectory mismatch. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 show that BudCache achieves better generation quality than heuristic caching baselines under the same inference budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache

中文摘要翻译: 步级缓存通过利用去噪步骤之间的时间冗余来加速扩散模型。现有方法使用基于阈值的启发式规则逐步做出缓存决策，没有直接优化最终输出质量。因此，它们的推理延迟随输入变化，在部署时难以控制。在这项工作中，我们提出了BudCache，它反转了这一公式：不是让逐步的错误阈值决定运行成本，而是预先固定计算预算，并搜索最能保留最终输出的缓存策略。为了应对步骤选择的组合复杂性，我们将模拟退火与确定性爬山算法相结合。这种离线搜索在几分钟内即可识别出高质量的缓存策略，并且在推理过程中不引入任何在线搜索或阈值开销。当计算预算非常紧张时，我们进一步引入缓存感知的调度对齐，根据所选缓存策略调整时间离散化，以减少缓存引起的轨迹失配。在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的实验表明，在相同推理预算下，BudCache的生成质量优于基于启发式的缓存基线方法。代码已开源在https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache。

</details>


### [61] [Heterogeneous LiDAR Early Fusion and Learned Re-Ranking Strategy for Robust Long-Term Place Recognition in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13503)
*Judith Vilella-Cantos,Juan José Cabrera,Mónica Ballesta,David Valiente,Luis Payá*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++，一种融合异质LiDAR数据（Livox Mid-360和Velodyne VLP-16）并结合学习重排序策略的农业环境地点识别方法，在葡萄园数据集上相比单传感器方法Recall@1提升20%，加入重排序后提升30%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 非结构化环境（如农业领域）中的鲁棒定位是自动驾驶系统的关键挑战，尤其在重复纹理的葡萄园环境中，传统单传感器方法难以有效区分正负样本。

Method: 提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++方法，包含两部分：(1) 早期融合两异构LiDAR传感器（Livox Mid-360和Velodyne VLP-16）的数据，利用各自优势提供更全面的环境表示；(2) 在推理阶段引入学习式重排序策略，提升重复环境中正确正样本的检索能力。

Result: 在TEMPO-VINE数据集上，相比单传感器方法，Recall@1指标提升20%，加入重排序后提升30%，性能超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: MinkUNeXt-VINE++通过异构LiDAR融合与学习重排序策略显著提升了农业非结构化环境中的地点识别性能，代码已开源。

Original Abstract: Robust localization in unstructured environments, such as agricultural fields, is a critical challenge for autonomous systems. LiDAR sensors provide detailed 3D information about the environment and are invariant to lighting conditions. For this reason, LiDAR-based place recognition methods have gained significant attention. In this paper, we propose MinkUNeXt-VINE++, a novel approach that combines early fusion of heterogeneous LiDAR data from two sensors (Livox Mid-360 and Velodyne VLP-16) and a learned re-ranking strategy in inference time. This fusion leverages the strengths of each sensor to provide a more comprehensive representation of the environment. Additionally, the re-ranking approach is particularly important in repetitive environments, such as vineyards, as finding true positives is a major challenge. We evaluated our approach using the TEMPO-VINE dataset, which provides heterogeneous LiDAR data in vineyard environments across different phenological stages. Our results demonstrate that MinkUNeXt-VINE++ significantly improves place recognition performance compared to single-sensor approaches and state-of-the-art methods. MinkUNeXt-VINE++ achieves a 20% improvement in the Recall@1 metric compared to single-sensor approaches, and +30% including re-ranking. The code of our method is publicly available for reproduction.

中文摘要翻译: 在非结构化环境（如农业田地）中实现鲁棒定位是自主系统面临的关键挑战。LiDAR传感器能提供环境的详细三维信息，且不受光照条件影响。因此，基于LiDAR的地点识别方法受到了广泛关注。本文提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++，一种将来自两个传感器（Livox Mid-360和Velodyne VLP-16）的异构LiDAR数据进行早期融合，并在推理阶段结合学习式重排序策略的新方法。该融合利用了各传感器的优势，以提供更全面的环境表示。此外，重排序方法在重复环境中（如葡萄园）尤为重要，因为在此类场景中寻找真正例是一项重大挑战。我们使用TEMPO-VINE数据集评估了所提方法，该数据集提供了不同物候阶段的葡萄园环境中的异构LiDAR数据。结果表明，与单传感器方法和现有最先进方法相比，MinkUNeXt-VINE++显著提升了地点识别性能。在Recall@1指标上，MinkUNeXt-VINE++相比单传感器方法提升了20%，加入重排序后提升了30%。我们的方法代码已公开，以供复现。

</details>


### [62] [Measurement-Calibrated Multi-Camera Fusion for Vision-Based Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13509)
*Mateo Toro Diz,Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测量校准的多摄像头融合方法，通过显式表征单摄像头定位误差（包括单应性标定、人体检测和运动跟踪）来优化数据融合。实验表明，该方法在绝对精度上提升有限，但能显著降低轨迹方差并改善运动平滑性，适用于需要稳定连续运动估计的应用场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于视觉的室内定位系统受检测噪声、遮挡和摄像头覆盖范围限制，存在多阶段不确定性。多摄像头数据融合通常被当作黑盒组件进行端到端评估，其机制性贡献不明确。本文旨在探究显式表征单摄像头定位误差是否可用于校准和优化多摄像头数据融合。

Method: 提出一种测量校准的融合方法（measurement-calibrated fusion），集成组件级误差量化，分别隔离和量化单应性标定、人体检测和运动跟踪三个环节的误差贡献，并基于此进行多摄像头数据融合优化。

Result: 数据融合相比单摄像头基线提升了定位精度；测量校准融合相比标准融合在绝对精度上提升有限，但显著降低了轨迹方差并改善了运动平滑性。

Conclusion: 显式误差表征在设计基于视觉的室内定位系统的数据融合策略中具有重要价值，尤其对于需要稳定连续运动估计的应用场景。

Original Abstract: Indoor vision-based localization systems are affected by detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage, leading to uncertainty at multiple stages of the pipeline. While multi-camera data fusion is widely used to mitigate these issues, it is typically treated as a black-box component and evaluated solely end-to-end, obscuring its mechanistic contributions. To address this gap, this work investigates whether explicitly characterizing single-camera localization errors can be leveraged to calibrate and optimize multi-camera data fusion.
  We introduce a measurement-calibrated fusion approach that integrates component-wise error quantification, specifically isolating homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. A component-wise evaluation is conducted to quantify error contributions from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking.
  Experimental results show that data fusion improves localization accuracy compared to single-camera baselines. While measurement-calibrated fusion provides only limited improvement in absolute accuracy over standard fusion, it substantially reduces trajectory variance and improves motion smoothness, which are critical for applications requiring stable and continuous motion estimates. These results highlight the value of explicit error characterization when designing data fusion strategies for vision-based indoor positioning systems.

中文摘要翻译: 基于视觉的室内定位系统受到检测噪声、遮挡以及摄像头覆盖范围有限的影���，导致流水线多个阶段存在不确定性。虽然多摄像头数据融合被广泛用于缓解这些问题，但它通常被视为黑盒组件，仅通过端到端方式评估，掩盖了其机制性贡献。为填补这一空白，本文研究了是否可以利用显式表征单摄像头定位误差来校准和优化多摄像头数据融合。我们提出了一种测量校准的融合方法，该方法集成了组件级误差量化，具体隔离了单应性标定、人体检测和运动跟踪的误差。通过组件级评估，量化了单应性标定、人体检测和运动跟踪的误差贡献。实验结果表明，与单摄像头基线相比，数据融合提高了定位精度。虽然测量校准融合在绝对精度上相比标准融合提升有限，但它显著降低了轨迹方差并改善了运动平滑性，这对于需要稳定且连续运动估计的应用至关重要。这些结果凸显了在设计基于视觉的室内定位系统的数据融合策略时进行显式误差表征的价值。

</details>


### [63] [MaskWAM: Unifying Mask Prompting and Prediction for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13515)
*Hanyang Yu,Haitao Lin,Jingbo Zhang,Wenyao Zhang,Chenghao Gu,Heng Li,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskWAM通过统一的混合Transformer架构将掩码作为显式输入和预测目标，解决了世界动作模型中的空间模糊和语义偏差问题，显著提升了机器人操纵策略的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）存在两个根本性空间瓶颈：标准文本输入在杂乱场景中引入指代模糊性，非结构化的RGB预测缺乏语义锚定且受任务无关背景干扰。

Method: 提出MaskWAM，采用以对象为中心的世界动作模型，通过统一的混合Transformer（MoT）架构将掩码同时作为显式输入和预测目标，实现对象中心的语义监督与精确空间锚定。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务上，MaskWAM在语言清晰和语言模糊两种任务设置下均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 掩码条件化作为视觉驱动的WAM架构的直接输入，比文本提供更强有力的引导，为操纵未见对象建立了一个精确且鲁棒的范式。

Original Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) present a promising paradigm for robotic control via video prediction. However, current WAMs suffer from fundamental spatial bottlenecks: standard text inputs introduce referential ambiguity in cluttered scenes, while unstructured RGB predictions lack semantic grounding and remain biased by task-irrelevant backgrounds. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MaskWAM, an object-centric world-action model. By jointly integrating masks as both explicit inputs and predictions via a unified Mixture of Transformers (MoT), MaskWAM unlocks robust policy generalization. This design provides two key benefits: (1) predicting future masks yields object-centric semantic supervision that suppresses visual noise, significantly enhancing even standard text-conditioned WAMs; and (2) coupling this predictive supervision with first-frame visual prompts, such as target object masks, establishes a precise spatial anchor that substantially reduces language ambiguity. Crucially, as WAMs are inherently vision-driven architectures, direct mask conditioning yields substantially stronger guidance than text alone, establishing a precise and robust paradigm for manipulating unseen objects. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks demonstrate that MaskWAM significantly outperforms baselines in both language-clear and language-ambiguous tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过视频预测为机器人控制提供了一种有前景的范式。然而，当前的WAMs存在根本性的空间瓶颈：标准文本输入在杂乱场景中引入指代模糊性，而非结构化的RGB预测缺乏语义锚定，且受到任务无关背景的偏差影响。为克服这些限制，我们提出了MaskWAM，一种以对象为中心的世界动作模型。通过统一的混合Transformer（MoT）架构将掩码同时作为显式输入和预测目标，MaskWAM实现了鲁棒的策略泛化。该设计提供两个关键优势：（1）预测未来掩码产生对象中心的语义监督，抑制视觉噪声，显著提升甚至标准文本条件化的WAMs；以及（2）将该预测监督与首帧视觉提示（如目标对象掩码）相结合，建立精确的空间锚定，大幅降低语言模糊性。关键在于，由于WAMs本质上是视觉驱动的架构，直接的掩码条件化比文本单独提供更强有力的引导，为操纵未见对象建立了一个精确且鲁棒的范式。在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务上的评估表明，MaskWAM在语言清晰和语言模糊两种任务中均显著优于基线方法。

</details>


### [64] [What's Old is New Again: Classical Dimensionality Reduction for Efficient Saliency-Guided Biometric Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13528)
*Samuel Webster,Walter Scheirer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于PCA和LDA降维技术生成显著性图的方法，无需人工标注即可用于生物特征呈现攻击检测（PAD）的显著性引导训练，在多个PAD领域超过基线甚至SOTA方法，解决了成本高、可扩展性差的障碍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有显著性引导训练在生物特征PAD中虽有优势，但显著性获取成本高、领域特定性强、可扩展性有限（如需要人工标注），亟需一种低成本、高可扩展性的显著性获取方法。

Method: 利用经典降维技术PCA和LDA直接从原始训练数据生成显著性图，无需人工标注或领域知识，将降维映射作为显著性信息来源指导模型训练。

Result: 在三个已有显著性探索的领域（虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD）和两个新的领域（指静脉PAD、身份证PAD）中，基于降维显著性图训练的模型均超过基线方法，部分达到甚至超越SOTA，且无需资源投入或领域特定工具。

Conclusion: 降维方法生成的显著性图是一种成本高效且高度可扩展的替代方案，克服了显著性引导训练在生物特征攻击检测及更广领域中应用的关键障碍。

Original Abstract: Saliency-guided training is a paradigm in visual recognition that encourages models to focus on the most relevant image regions during learning. While its application in biometric presentation attack detection (PAD) has shown strong benefits in robustness and generalization, adoption is often limited by the high cost, domain specificity, and limited scalability of existing saliency acquisition methods, such as human annotations over a limited dataset. We present a novel, cost-efficient, and highly-scalable approach to saliency acquisition using maps inspired by classical dimensionality reduction techniques: PCA and LDA. Our proposed methods generate saliency maps directly from raw training data, requiring no human annotation nor domain knowledge. We contextualize the effectiveness of these saliency sources in three saliency-explored domains (iris PAD, synthetic face detection, fingerprint PAD) and demonstrate its scalability in two saliency-novel domains (fingerprint vein PAD and ID card PAD). Across all domains tested, models trained using dimensionality reduction-sourced saliency maps exceed baseline and sometimes SOTA saliency methods without any resource investment or domain-specific tooling. Our findings overcome an important yet unaddressed barrier to saliency-guided training for biometric attack detection and beyond.

中文摘要翻译: 显著性引导训练是视觉识别中的一种范式，它鼓励模型在学习过程中关注最具相关性的图像区域。虽然在生物特征呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的应用在鲁棒性和泛化性方面表现出显著优势，但现有的显著性获取方法（如在有限数据集上进行人工标注）成本高昂、领域特异性强且可扩展性有限，限制了其广泛采用。我们提出了一种新颖、成本高效且高度可扩展的显著性获取方法，利用经典降维技术——PCA和LDA——生成的显著性图。所提出的方法直接从原始训练数据生成显著性图，无需人工标注或领域知识。我们在三个已有显著性探索的领域（虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD）中验证了这些显著性源的有效性，并在两个新的显著性领域（指静脉PAD和身份证PAD）中展示了其可扩展性。在所有测试领域中，使用降维源显著性图训练的模型均超过基线方法，部分甚至达到或超越了当前最优（SOTA）的显著性方法，且无需任何资源投入或领域特定工具。我们的研究发现克服了显著性引导训练在生物特征攻击检测及更广领域中应用的一个尚未解决的重要障碍。

</details>


### [65] [Edit the Bits, Diff the Codes: Bitwise Residual Editing for Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13558)
*Shengqiang Zhang,Ruotong Liao,Volker Tresp,Barbara Plank,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BitResEdit，一种无需训练的图像编辑方法，通过位级引导（BitEdit）和残差组合（ResEdit）两个互补模块，在VAR生成器（如Infinity）上实现文本引导的图像编辑，在PIE-Bench上达到最优文本对齐效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VAR图像编辑器主要操作在token流、特征或扁平化的next-token logits上，未能充分利用位级残差VAR模型的两个原生结构：逐位的伯努利预测头和加法多尺度残差编码场。作者希望利用这些被忽视的结构实现更优的图像编辑。

Method: 提出两个核心模块：1) BitEdit：通过源-负向引导，在共享编辑前缀上计算源-目标对比，倾斜CFG后的逐位对数几率，并将更新投影到闭式伯努利KL信任域内；2) ResEdit：将采样到的位转换为每尺度连续码残差，用定位掩码门控，通过生成器的原生求和尺度机制重新注入。两者协同实现决策时位引导与组合时代码组合的耦合。

Result: 在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B模型，BitResEdit在相同骨干网络的VAR编辑器中实现了最强的文本对齐性能，在编辑区域上的CLIP分数比最强先验编辑器高+1.07，同时背景保持性能与之相当。

Conclusion: BitEdit和ResEdit在目标对齐和背景保持方面扮演互补角色，消融实验验证了这一点。该方法无需训练即可有效工作。

Original Abstract: Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.

中文摘要翻译: 基于视觉自回归（VAR）生成器的文本引导图像编辑需要控制模型采样的内容以及采样变更写回图像编码的位置。现有的VAR编辑器主要操作于token流、特征或扁平化的next-token logits上，未能充分利用位级残差VAR模型的两个原生结构：逐位的伯努利预测头和图像由之组合而成的加法多尺度残差编码场。我们提出BitResEdit，一种针对位级残差VAR生成器（如Infinity）的免训练编辑器。BitEdit通过沿共享编辑前缀上计算的源-目标对比倾斜CFG后的逐位对数几率来执行源负向引导，然后将每次更新投影到干净CFG采样器周围的闭式伯努利KL信任域内。ResEdit将采样到的位转换为每尺度的连续码残差，用定位掩码门控，然后通过生成器的原生尺度求和机制重新注入。两者共同耦合了决策时的位引导与组合时的编码组合，使得被掩码的潜在特征通过编码算术被精确保留，同时在目标区域内应用局部的、尺度感知的编辑。在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B模型，BitResEdit在相同骨干网络的VAR编辑器中达到了最强的文本对齐性能，编辑区域上的CLIP分数比最强先验编辑器高出+1.07，同时背景保持性能与之相当。消融实验表明，BitEdit和ResEdit在目标对齐和背景保持方面发挥着互补作用。

</details>


### [66] [Contrast-Informed Augmentation and Domain-Adversarial Training for Adult-to-Neonatal MR Reconstruction Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13562)
*Stephen Moore,Lara Leijser,Richard Frayne,Roberto Souza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了对比度感知数据增强和领域对抗训练能否提升E2E-VarNet模型从成人到新生儿的泛化能力。结果表明，混合训练和混合领域对抗训练均优于纯成人数据训练，其中混合领域对抗训练在新生儿数据上取得了最佳性能，t-SNE可视化表明该方法增强了不同领域潜在表示的重叠。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度学习MRI重建模型在成人数据上表现良好，但直接应用于新生儿数据时因领域偏移（domain shift）而泛化性能下降，亟需提升模型从成人到新生儿数据领域迁移的鲁棒性。

Method: 对比了三种训练策略：(1) 仅用未增强成人数据训练（Unaug-Only）；(2) 混合未增强成人数据与新生儿感知增强成人数据的混合训练（Mixed）；(3) 在混合训练基础上加入领域对抗目标（Mixed-DAT）。模型在回顾性欠采样的多线圈成人T2加权脑MR数据上训练，在新生儿和成人测试数据上评估。

Result: 在R=4时，Mixed-DAT综合最优（SSIM=0.924, PSNR=33.98 dB）；在R=8时，Mixed-DAT在SSIM上最优（0.848），Mixed在PSNR上最优（29.56 dB）。Mixed-DAT还增加了未增强成人、增强成人和新生儿测试数据潜在表示的t-SNE重叠程度。

Conclusion: 对比度感知数据增强结合领域对抗训练能显著提升深度学习MRI重建从成人到新生儿领域的泛化能力，有望提高欠采样新生儿MRI重建对领域偏移的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Purpose: To investigate whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training improve the adult-to-neonatal generalization of the E2E-VarNet. Methods: Three training regimes were investigated: (1) adult-only training with unaugmented adult data, (2) mixed training with paired unaugmented and neonatal-informed augmented adult data, and (3) mixed training with a domain-adversarial objective. Models were trained on retrospectively undersampled multi-coil adult T2-weighted brain MR data and evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors $R=4$ and $R=8$ using quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation. Feature analyses assessed whether domain-adversarial training altered the latent representations of unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test samples. Results: Mixed training (Mixed) and mixed domain-adversarial training (Mixed-DAT) outperformed unaugmented adult-only training (Unaug-Only) when evaluated on neonatal data. At R=4, Mixed-DAT achieved the best performance (SSIM = 0.924 +/- 0.027, PSNR = 33.98 +/- 1.15 dB). At R=8, Mixed-DAT performed best when measured using SSIM (0.848 +/- 0.031 vs. 0.766 +/- 0.037 for Unaug-Only and 0.814 +/- 0.035 for Mixed) and Mixed performed best when measured using PSNR (29.56 +/- 0.83 dB vs. 26.26 +/- 0.78 dB for Unaug-Only and 29.43 +/- 0.83 dB for Mixed-DAT). Qualitative assessment of t-SNE plots suggested that Mixed-DAT increased the overlap among the latent representations of the unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test data. Conclusion: Contrast-informed augmentation and domain-adversarial training improved adult-to-neonatal generalization of deep learning-based MR reconstruction. These findings suggest that contrast-informed data augmentation combined with adversarial training may improve robustness to domain shift in undersampled neonatal MR reconstruction.

中文摘要翻译: 目的：探究对比度感知数据增强与领域对抗训练是否能够提升E2E-VarNet从成人到新生儿数据的泛化能力。方法：研究了三种训练策略：(1) 仅使用未增强的成人数据进行训练，(2) 混合使用配对未增强数据和新生儿感知增强的成人数据进行训练，(3) 使用包含领域对抗目标的混合训练。模型在回顾性欠采样的多线圈成人T2加权脑MR数据上训练，并在加速因子R=4和R=8下使用定量指标和定性评估在新生儿和成人测试数据上进行评价。特征分析评估了领域对抗训练是否改变了未增强成人、增强成人及新生儿测试样本的潜在表示。结果：混合训练（Mixed）和混合领域对抗训练（Mixed-DAT）在新生儿数据评估中均优于仅用未增强成人数据训练（Unaug-Only）。在R=4时，Mixed-DAT取得了最佳性能（SSIM=0.924 ± 0.027，PSNR=33.98 ± 1.15 dB）。在R=8时，Mixed-DAT在SSIM指标上表现最优（0.848 ± 0.031，对比Unaug-Only的0.766 ± 0.037和Mixed的0.814 ± 0.035），而Mixed在PSNR指标上表现最佳（29.56 ± 0.83 dB，对比Unaug-Only的26.26 ± 0.78 dB和Mixed-DAT的29.43 ± 0.83 dB）。t-SNE图的定性评估表明，Mixed-DAT增加了未增强成人、增强成人和新生儿测试数据潜在表示之间的重叠程度。结论：对比度感知增强与领域对抗训练改善了深度学习MRI重建从成人到新生儿的泛化能力。这些发现表明，对比度感知数据增强结合对抗训练可能提高欠采样新生儿MRI重建对领域偏移的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [67] [EvTexture++: Event-Driven Texture Enhancement for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13580)
*Dachun Kai,Jiayao Lu,Yueyi Zhang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvTexture++是首个面向视频超分辨率（VSR）中纹理增强的事件驱动框架，利用事件的高时空分辨率信息，通过迭代纹理增强和时序纹理对齐模块，实现更优的纹理恢复与时间一致性，作为即插即用模块可显著提升现有VSR模型性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有事件驱动VSR工作主要将事件信号用于运动细化（如光流估计和时间对齐），而忽略了事件在纹理增强方面的潜力。本文旨在将事件信号的焦点从运动细化转向纹理增强，以提升VSR中纹理区域的恢复质量。

Method: EvTexture++包含三个核心组件：（1）纹理增强分支，专用于纹理恢复；（2）迭代纹理增强模块，逐步利用高时间分辨率的事件信息渐进式细化纹理区域；（3）时序纹理对齐模块，利用事件的连续时间运动线索估计事件引导的纹理感知光流，实现帧间纹理对齐，缓解大运动导致的纹理闪烁。整体框架设计为即插即用模块，可灵活提升现有VSR模型性能。

Result: 在五个数据集上达到最先进性能。集成到现有VSR模型中后，在纹理丰富的Vid4数据集上PSNR提升最高达1.55 dB。

Conclusion: 事件信号在VSR中不仅能用于运动细化，更能有效用于纹理增强。EvTexture++作为首个专门针对纹理增强的事件驱动VSR框架，证明了事件信息在纹理恢复和时间一致性方面的巨大潜力，且作为即插即用模块具有广泛适用性。

Original Abstract: Event-based vision has drawn increasing attention owing to its distinctive properties, including ultra-high temporal resolution and extreme dynamic range. Recent works have introduced it to video super-resolution (VSR) to enhance flow estimation and temporal alignment. In contrast, this paper shifts the focus of event signals from motion refinement to texture enhancement in VSR. We propose EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework dedicated to texture enhancement in VSR. It leverages high-frequency spatiotemporal details from events to improve texture recovery. EvTexture++ incorporates a customized texture enhancement branch, along with an iterative texture enhancement module that progressively exploits high-temporal-resolution event information for texture restoration. This enables gradual refinement of texture regions across iterations, yielding more accurate and detailed high-resolution outputs. Besides intra-frame texture recovery, large motions could degrade inter-frame temporal consistency, particularly in texture regions, leading to texture flickering. To mitigate this, we further exploit the continuous-time motion cues of events to enhance temporal consistency, introducing a temporal texture alignment module that estimates event-guided texture-aware flow for precise inter-frame texture alignment. Moreover, EvTexture++ is designed as a plug-and-play tool to flexibly boost the performance of existing VSR models. Experiments on five datasets demonstrate that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art performance. When integrated into recent VSR models, it yields significant improvements, with gains of up to 1.55 dB in PSNR on the texture-rich Vid4 dataset. Code: https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture.

中文摘要翻译: 基于事件的视觉因其独特特性（包括超高时间分辨率和极端动态范围）而受到越来越多的关注。近期工作已将其引入视频超分辨率（VSR）中，以增强光流估计和时间对齐。相比之下，本文将事件信号的关注点从VSR中的运动细化转向纹理增强。我们提出了EvTexture++，这是首个专用于VSR中纹理增强的事件驱动框架。它利用事件的高频时空细节来改善纹理恢复。EvTexture++包含一个定制的纹理增强分支，以及一个迭代纹理增强模块，该模块逐步利用高时间分辨率的事件信息进行纹理恢复。这实现了跨迭代逐步细化纹理区域，从而产生更精确、更详细的高分辨率输出。除了帧内纹理恢复外，大运动可能会降低帧间时间一致性，尤其是在纹理区域，导致纹理闪烁。为缓解这一问题，我们进一步利用事件的连续时间运动线索来增强时间一致性，引入了一个时序纹理对齐模块，该模块估计事件引导的纹理感知光流，以实现精确的帧间纹理对齐。此外，EvTexture++被设计为一个即插即用工具，可灵活提升现有VSR模型的性能。在五个数据集上的实验表明，EvTexture++实现了最先进的性能。当集成到最近的VSR模型中时，它带来了显著的改进，在纹理丰富的Vid4数据集上PSNR增益高达1.55 dB。代码：https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture。

</details>


### [68] [Towards Effective Waste Segmentation for Automated Waste Recycling in Cluttered Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13587)
*Mamoona Javaid,Mubashir Noman,Abdul Hannan,Shah Nawaz,Mustansar Fiaz,Sajid Ghuffar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种级联式最优垃圾分割网络，通过联合利用空间域和频域信息，并引入辅助特征增强模块（AFEM），在ZeroWaste和SpectralWaste数据集上实现了更优的垃圾分割性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的垃圾分割方法依赖大型骨干网络，在资源受限的自动垃圾回收系统中效率低下，且在杂乱场景中性能下降严重。

Method: 提出一种级联式最优垃圾分割网络，有效利用空间域捕获局部结构依赖关系，利用频域提取全局上下文关系，通过级联设计逐步融合局部与全局表征；同时引入辅助特征增强模块（AFEM）来增强目标物体的边界和斑点放大，以改善杂乱场景下的分割效果。

Result: 在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste三个数据集上的大量实验证明了所提方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的级联式垃圾分割网络结合空间域和频域信息以及AFEM模块，能够高效且准确地在杂乱场景中分割各类垃圾物体，适用于自动垃圾回收系统。

Original Abstract: Rapid expansion of urban areas and population growth is causing an immense increase in waste production, which demands the need for efficient and automated waste management. In this scenario, automated waste recycling (AWR) using deep learning methods can assist humans in optimal waste management. Recent deep learning approaches for AWR provide promising waste segmentation performance, however, these methods rely on large backbone networks that are inefficient for AWR systems and suffer from performance deterioration in cluttered scenes. To this end, an optimal waste segmentation network is introduced which effectively utilizes the spatial domain to capture localized structural dependencies and the spectral domain to efficiently extract global contextual relationships. This cascaded design allows the network to progressively leverage both local and global representations across complementary domains to highlight the semantic information necessary for effective segmentation of various waste objects. Furthermore, auxiliary feature enhancement module (AFEM) is introduced to enhance the target objects' boundaries and blob amplification for better segmentation in cluttered scenarios. Extensive experimentation on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f and SpectralWaste datasets reveals the merits of the proposed method.

中文摘要翻译: 城市区域的快速扩张和人口增长正导致垃圾产量急剧增加，这要求高效且自动化的垃圾管理。在此背景下，使用深度学习方法的自动化垃圾回收（AWR）能够帮助人类实现最优的垃圾管理。近期用于AWR的深度学习方法提供了有前景的垃圾分割性能，然而，这些方法依赖大型骨干网络，这对AWR系统效率较低，且在杂乱场景中会出现性能退化。为此，本文引入了一种最优垃圾分割网络，该网络有效利用空间域来捕获局部结构依赖关系，并利用频域来高效提取全局上下文关系。这种级联设计使得网络能够逐步利用互补域上的局部和全局表征，突出有效分割各类垃圾物体所需的语义信息。此外，本文还引入了辅助特征增强模块（AFEM），用于增强目标物体的边界和斑点放大，以在杂乱场景中获得更好的分割效果。在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste数据集上的大量实验揭示了所提方法的优越性。

</details>


### [69] [Revisiting Vehicle Color Recognition in Long-Tailed Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13625)
*Vinícius Orrú,Bruno H. Foggiatto,Gabriel E. Lima,David Menotti,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了监控场景下严重类别不平衡的车辆颜色识别问题，通过合成少数类数据增强、现代视觉表征、损失重加权等策略，在UFPR-VeSV数据集上将宏平均准确率提升8.2个百分点至79.7%，并指出许多剩余错误对人工标注者而言也存在视觉歧义。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实监控场景中车辆颜色分布高度不平衡，现有总体准确率指标无法反映稀有但实际重要的颜色类别的识别性能，需在严重类别不平衡条件下系统研究车辆颜色识别方法。

Method: 结合两类生成式合成数据增强策略（RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL的文本条件图像生成和Gemini 2.0 Flash的图像条件颜色编辑），配合现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全的数据增强、前景感知预处理及集成融合。

Result: 最佳方法达到94.6%微平均准确率和79.7%宏平均准确率，宏平均准确率相比近期文献提升8.2个百分点；人工错误分析表明许多剩余失败对人工标注者而言也存在视觉歧义。

Conclusion: 合成少数类数据增强能有效缓解车辆颜色识别中的类别不平衡问题，但无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别存在实际局限性，部分视觉歧义难以完全消除。

Original Abstract: Vehicle color recognition is an important cue for vehicle identification in surveillance systems, especially when license plates are illegible due to low resolution, occlusion, motion blur, or poor illumination. However, real-world vehicle color distributions are highly imbalanced, making overall accuracy insufficient to assess performance on rare but operationally relevant colors. This paper presents a comprehensive study of vehicle color recognition under severe class imbalance using UFPR-VeSV, a challenging real-world surveillance dataset. We investigate synthetic minority-class augmentation through two off-the-shelf generative strategies: text-conditioned image generation with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. The curated synthetic data are combined with modern visual representations, loss reweighting, learning-rate scheduling, color-safe augmentation, foreground-aware preprocessing, and ensemble fusion. The bestperforming approach achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, improving macro accuracy by 8.2 percentage points over recent literature. A manual error analysis further shows that many remaining failures are visually ambiguous even for human annotators, highlighting the practical limits of color-based vehicle identification in unconstrained surveillance imagery. The generated images and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic

中文摘要翻译: 车辆颜色识别是监控系统中车辆识别的重要线索，特别是在车牌因低分辨率、遮挡、运动模糊或光照不足而无法辨认的情况下。然而，真实场景中的车辆颜色分布高度不平衡，导致总体准确率不足以评估稀有但实际相关的颜色的性能。本文利用具有挑战性的真实监控数据集UFPR-VeSV，对严重类别不平衡下的车辆颜色识别进行了全面研究。我们通过两种现成的生成式策略探索了合成少数类数据增强：使用RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL的文本条件图像生成和使用Gemini 2.0 Flash的图像条件颜色编辑。经过筛选的合成数据与现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全的数据增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合相结合。表现最佳的方法达到了94.6%的微平均准确率和79.7%的宏平均准确率，宏平均准确率相比近期文献提升了8.2个百分点。人工错误分析进一步表明，许多剩余失败案例即使对于人工标注者而言也存在视觉歧义，这突显了在无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别的实际局限性。生成的图像和源代码已公开于https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic。

</details>


### [70] [Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13644)
*Antoine Guédon,Shu Nakamura,Nicolas Dufour,Jiahui Lei,Ko Nishino,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Surflo提出了一种基于流匹配的3D曲面点解码方法，将任意数量的未位姿RGB视图压缩为K个潜变量令牌（全局状态），并独立地将噪声点传输到曲面，支持从数千到百万个点的任意分辨率解码，性能匹配或超越前馈基线方法，速度比优化方法快一个数量级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型存在两类缺陷：逐视角方法生成重叠且未对齐的点云，输出随输入数量线性增长；全局潜变量方法则受限于固定的低分辨率输出。两者都未能利用几何对视角不变性的特性，即多视图本质上是单个3D状态的冗余编码。

Method: 1) 将可变数量的未位姿RGB视图压缩为K个潜变量令牌（全局状态）；2) 通过流匹配（flow matching）将噪声点独立传输到3D曲面；3) 引入推理时引导项（guidance term），在ODE积分过程中注入光度梯度来关联相邻点，抑制独立逐点解码导致的局部不一致性。

Result: 在曲面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线方法；运行速度比需要数百张视图的优化方法快一个数量级；是唯一同时具备全局潜变量与任意分辨率解码能力的前馈方法。

Conclusion: Surflo成功实现了全局潜变量表示与任意分辨率曲面解码的结合，验证了流匹配在3D重建中独立逐点解码路径的可行性，并通过引导项有效解决了局部一致性问题。

Original Abstract: Geometry is invariant to viewpoint, which makes any collection of images a redundant encoding of a single 3D state. Existing feed-forward reconstruction models fail to exploit this: per-view methods emit overlapping, unaligned pointmaps that grow linearly with input count, while global-latent methods commit to a fixed, low-resolution output. We introduce Surflo, which compresses a variable number of unposed RGB views into K latent tokens-one global state-and decodes oriented 3D surface points by independently transporting them from noise onto the surface via flow matching. This frees the output from any fixed grid or token budget: the same latent yields from a few thousand to a million points in a single forward pass. To suppress the local inconsistencies inherent to independent per-point decoding, an inference-time guidance term correlates nearby points by injecting a photometric gradient during ODE integration. Surflo matches or surpasses feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs an order of magnitude faster than optimization-based methods that require hundreds of views, and is the only feed-forward approach to combine a global latent with arbitrary-resolution decoding.

中文摘要翻译: 几何结构对视角具有不变性，这使得任意图像集合都是单个3D状态的冗余编码。现有的前馈重建模型未能充分利用这一点：逐视角方法生成重叠且未对齐的点云，其输出随输入数量线性增长；而全局潜变量方法则受限于固定的低分辨率输出。我们提出了Surflo，它将可变数量的未位姿RGB视图压缩为K个潜变量令牌——即一个全局状态——并通过流匹配将噪声点独立传输到曲面，从而解码出带方向的3D曲面点。这使得输出不受任何固定网格或令牌预算的限制：相同的潜变量可在单次前向传播中生成从数千到百万个点。为了抑制独立逐点解码固有的局部不一致性，我们引入了一项推理时引导项，通过在ODE积分过程中注入光度梯度来关联相邻点。Surflo在曲面指标上匹配或超越了前馈基线方法，运行速度比需要数百张视图的优化方法快一个数量级，并且是唯一同时结合全局潜变量与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。

</details>


### [71] [World Tracing: Generative Pixel-Aligned Geometry Beyond the Visible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13652)
*Hao Zhang,Mohamed El Banani,Jen-Hao Cheng,Paul Zhang,Yi Hua,Ben Mildenhall,Christoph Lassner,Narendra Ahuja,Gengshan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: World Tracing 提出了一种生成式像素对齐几何表示方法，通过预测每个像素对应的有序3D点堆栈（可见表面+遮挡表面），并基于扩散Transformer（WT-DiT）实现，在可见表面重建和完整几何生成上均优于现有方法，同时支持多种下游任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像到3D方法在保真度与完整性之间存在权衡：深度估计器忠实于输入像素但仅能重建可见表面，而生成式模型能生成完整形状但常与输入不对齐。本文旨在同时实现像素对齐的可见表面重建与合理的遮挡几何补全。

Method: 提出World Tracing表示，为每个输入像素预测一个有序的相机空间3D点堆栈（第一层为可见表面，后续层为从前往后与遮挡表面的交点）。实例化为WT-DiT，一个世界追踪扩散Transformer，将多层几何视为独立的去噪token，通过分解注意力和全局注意力耦合，使用像素空间流匹配和混合噪声调度训练。

Result: 在物体、场景和动态数据的多个基准上，WT-DiT在可见表面重建和完整几何生成方面均优于深度预测器和图像到3D生成器，并保持了2D到3D的对应关系，支持文本驱动的3D场景编辑、几何条件的新视角视频合成，以及无需训练即可与纹理网格生成器集成。

Conclusion: World Tracing通过生成式像素对齐几何表示，成功弥合了深度估计与图像到3D生成之间的鸿沟，在保持像素级对齐的同时实现完整的几何补全，且具有良好的泛化能力和下游应用潜力。

Original Abstract: Image-to-3D methods often trade off faithfulness and completeness: depth estimators are anchored to input pixels but stop at the visible surface, while image-to-3D models generate complete shapes that are often misaligned with the input. We introduce World Tracing, a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with observed pixels while completing geometry beyond the visible surface. For each input pixel, World Tracing predicts an ordered stack of camera-space 3D points, where the first layer represents the visible surface and subsequent layers represent front-to-back intersections with occluded surfaces. We instantiate this representation with a world-tracing diffusion transformer, WT-DiT, which treats multiple geometry layers as separate denoising tokens coupled through factorized and global attention. WT-DiT is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule that balances visible-surface reconstruction with occluded-geometry generation. World Tracing achieves strong performance on visible-surface reconstruction and complete geometry generation across object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, outperforming both depth predictors and image-to-3D generators. It also preserves 2D-to-3D correspondence, enabling text-driven 3D scene editing, geometry-conditioned novel-view video synthesis, and training-free integration with textured-mesh generators.

中文摘要翻译: 图像到3D方法通常在保真度和完整性之间进行权衡：深度估计器锚定于输入像素，但止步于可见表面；而图像到3D模型能生成完整形状，却常与输入不对齐。我们提出World Tracing，一种生成式像素对齐几何表示，能够在预测与观测像素对齐的3D点的同时，补全超出可见表面的几何结构。对于每个输入像素，World Tracing预测一个有序的相机空间3D点堆栈，其中第一层表示可见表面，后续层表示从前到后与遮挡表面的交点。我们通过一个世界追踪扩散Transformer（WT-DiT）实例化该表示，该模型将多层几何视为独立的去噪token，并通过分解注意力和全局注意力进行耦合。WT-DiT采用像素空间流匹配和混合噪声调度进行训练，以平衡可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成。在物体、场景和动态数据的多个基准上，World Tracing在可见表面重建和完整几何生成方面均表现出色，优于深度预测器和图像到3D生成器。它还保持了2D到3D的对应关系，支持文本驱动的3D场景编辑、几何条件的新视角视频合成，以及无需训练即可与纹理网格生成器集成。

</details>


### [72] [Flex4DHuman: Flexible Multi-view Video Diffusion for 4D Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13655)
*Jen-Hao Cheng,Yipeng Wang,Hao Zhang,Gengshan Yang,Jenq-Neng Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flex4DHuman 是一种多视角视频扩散模型，仅需相对相机位姿条件，即可将单目或稀疏多视角视频转换为密集多视角视频，并可直接用于4D高斯溅射重建，无需显式几何先验。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于人体的动态4D重建方法往往依赖骨架、深度图、法线等显式几何先验，限制了泛化能力；同时，从单目视频直接生成动态4D内容仍具挑战。本文旨在提出无需显式几何先验、仅靠相机位姿条件即可生成密集多视角视频的统一框架。

Method: 基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频模型，保持其主干架构，通过五轴位置编码（在时空RoPE基础上扩展视图索引和连续SE(3)相对相机几何）编码相机和视图信息；采用三阶段课程训练：位姿跟随、灵活参考到目标视图生成、时间展开；训练中使用干净的历史目标视图token支持时间展开，并加入多视角字幕实现测试时文本控制。

Result: 在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ数据集上超过先前最先进方法；混合人类-动物训练后，同一公式可泛化到动物类别。

Conclusion: Flex4DHuman为从随意单目视频实现可扩展的4D内容创作提供了实用方案，适用于仿真、游戏、AR/VR和视频重拍等场景。

Original Abstract: We present Flex4DHuman, a multi-view video diffusion model that transforms a monocular or sparse multi-view video of a dynamic subject into synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning. Unlike prior human-centric methods that rely on skeletons, depth maps, normals, or rendered target-view geometry, Flex4DHuman requires no explicit geometry priors and instead conditions generation through relative camera-pose positional encoding. The generated videos can be directly ingested by downstream reconstruction pipelines to create dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Built on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video model, Flex4DHuman preserves the backbone architecture and encodes camera and view information through a five-axis positional encoding that extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) relative camera geometry. A three-stage curriculum progressively trains the model for pose following, flexible reference-to-target view generation, and temporal rollout. To support temporal rollout, we train with clean historical target-view tokens. We also add multi-view captions to enable test-time text control. Combined with an off-the-shelf 4D Gaussian Splatting stage, our framework lifts monocular static-camera videos into dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ show that Flex4DHuman surpasses prior state-of-the-art methods, while the same formulation generalizes to animal categories after mixed human-animal training. These capabilities make Flex4DHuman a practical step toward scalable 4D content creation from casual monocular videos for simulation, gaming, AR/VR, and video re-shooting.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Flex4DHuman，一种多视角视频扩散模型，它仅使用相对相机位姿条件，即可将动态主体的单目或稀疏多视角视频转换为同步的密集多视角视频。与先前依赖骨架、深度图、法线或渲染目标视图几何的人体中心方法不同，Flex4DHuman不需要显式几何先验，而是通过相对相机位姿位置编码来条件化生成过程。生成的视频可直接输入下游重建流程，创建动态4D高斯溅射。Flex4DHuman基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频模型构建，保留了主干架构，通过五轴位置编码（在时空RoPE基础上扩展了视图索引和连续SE(3)相对相机几何）来编码相机和视图信息。三阶段课程训练逐步训练模型实现位姿跟随、灵活的参考到目标视图生成以及时间展开。为支持时间展开，我们使用干净的历史目标视图token进行训练。我们还增加了多视角字幕以实现测试时的文本控制。结合现成的4D高斯溅射阶段，我们的框架将单目静态相机视频提升为动态4D高斯溅射。在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ上的实验表明，Flex4DHuman超越了先前最先进的方法，而相同的公式在混合人类-动物训练后可泛化到动物类别。这些能力使Flex4DHuman成为从随意单目视频实现可扩展4D内容创作的实用一步，适用于仿真、游戏、AR/VR和视频重拍。

</details>


### [73] [SpatialClaw: Rethinking Action Interface for Agentic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13673)
*Seokju Cho,Ryo Hachiuma,Abhishek Badki,Hang Su,Byung-Kwan Lee,Chan Hee Song,Sifei Liu,Subhashree Radhakrishnan,Seungryong Kim,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Min-Hung Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpatialClaw，一种无需训练的空间推理框架，将代码作为动作接口，通过有状态的Python内核支持逐步执行和灵活组合感知模块，在20个空间推理基准上平均准确率59.9%，超越先前方法11.2个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有空间智能体要么采用单次代码执行（观察中间结果前就确定完整策略），要么依赖结构化工具调用接口（灵活性不足），两者都难以支持开放式的复杂3D/4D空间推理。因此需要一种更灵活的动作接口设计。

Method: SpatialClaw采用代码作为动作接口，维护一个带状态的Python内核，预加载输入帧和一系列感知与几何原语。VLM驱动的智能体每步执行一个可执行代码单元，该单元基于所有先前输出，从而灵活组合和操作感知结果，并根据中间文本/视觉观察和问题需求自适应调整分析策略。

Result: 在20个涵盖静态和动态3D/4D空间推理任务的基准上，SpatialClaw平均准确率59.9%，比最新空间智能体高出11.2个百分点。在两种模型家族的六个VLM骨干上取得一致提升，无需任何基准或模型特定的适配。

Conclusion: 代码作为动作接口的设计，结合有状态逐步执行，能够显著提升VLM在开放空间推理任务中的灵活性和性能，是一种无需训练且通用性强的有效范式。

Original Abstract: Spatial reasoning, the ability to determine where objects are, how they relate, and how they move in 3D, remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). Tool-augmented agents attempt to address this by augmenting VLMs with specialist perception modules, yet their effectiveness is bounded by the action interface through which those tools are invoked. In this work, we study how the design of this interface shapes the agent's capacity for open-ended spatial reasoning. Existing spatial agents either employ single-pass code execution, which commits to a full analysis strategy before any intermediate result is observed, or rely on a structured tool-call interface that often offers less flexibility for freely composing operations or tailoring the analysis to each task. Both designs offer limited flexibility for open-ended, complex 3D/4D spatial reasoning. We therefore propose SpatialClaw, a training-free framework for spatial reasoning that adopts code as the action interface. SpatialClaw maintains a stateful Python kernel pre-loaded with input frames and a suite of perception and geometry primitives, letting a VLM-backed agent write one executable cell per step conditioned on all prior outputs, enabling the agent to flexibly compose and manipulate perception results and adapt its analysis to both intermediate text and visual observations and the demands of each problem. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks spanning a broad range of static and dynamic 3D/4D spatial reasoning tasks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the recent spatial agent by +11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two model families without any benchmark- or model-specific adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 空间推理——即判断物体在哪里、它们如何关联以及在三维空间中如何运动的能力——仍然是视觉-语言模型（VLM）面临的一个基本挑战。工具增强型智能体试图通过为VLM配备专门的感知模块来解决这一问题，但其有效性受限于调用这些工具的动作接口。在这项工作中，我们研究了接口设计如何影响智能体进行开放式空间推理的能力。现有空间智能体要么采用单次代码执行（在观察到任何中间结果之前就提交完整的分析策略），要么依赖结构化的工具调用接口（通常在自由组合操作或为每个任务定制分析方面灵活性较差）。这两种设计对于开放式的复杂3D/4D空间推理都提供了有限的灵活性。因此，我们提出SpatialClaw，一种无需训练的空间推理框架，采用代码作为动作接口。SpatialClaw维护一个有状态的Python内核，其中预加载了输入帧以及一套感知和几何原语，使得由VLM驱动的智能体能够每步基于所有先前的输出编写一个可执行的代码单元，从而实现灵活组合和操作感知结果，并根据中间文本和视觉观察以及每个问题的需求自适应调整分析。在涵盖广泛静态和动态3D/4D空间推理任务的20个空间推理基准上评估，SpatialClaw达到了59.9%的平均准确率，比最新的空间智能体高出11.2个百分点，并且在两个模型家族的六个VLM骨干上均取得了一致的性能提升，无需任何基准或模型特定的适配。

</details>


### [74] [RepWAM: World Action Modeling with Representation Visual-Action Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13674)
*Junke Wang,Qihang Zhang,Shuai Yang,Yiming Luo,Yujun Shen,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RepWAM提出了一种基于表征性视觉-动作分词器的世界动作模型，通过对齐的语义潜在空间联合建模未来视觉状态与潜在动作，在真实操作任务和仿真基准上取得了优异表现，证明了表征性视觉-动作分词化作为世界动作模型基础的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）通常继承自视频生成模型的面向重建的分词器，虽然保持了视觉保真度，但像素级重建对学习连接未来预测与机器人控制的指令跟随动态提供的指导有限。因此需要探索一个语义化的视觉-动作潜在空间来实现表征中心的动作世界建模。

Method: 1) 训练一个表征性视觉-动作分词器，将视觉输入映射为对齐的视觉token和潜在动作token；2) 基于该分词器预训练世界动作模型，在语言指令下联合建模未来视觉状态及其之间的潜在动作；3) 通过真实机器人轨迹进行适配，实现闭环操作控制。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务和仿真基准上的实验表明，RepWAM在多种操作场景下均展现了强大的性能；消融实验进一步凸显了语义化视觉-动作分词化相对于面向重建替代方案的优势。

Conclusion: 表征性视觉-动作分词化是构建世界动作模型的有效基础，是迈向通用机器人策略的重要一步。

Original Abstract: This work presents RepWAM, a representation-centric world action model (WAM) built on representation visual-action tokenizers. Existing WAMs typically inherit reconstruction-oriented video tokenizers from pretrained video generation models. Although these tokenizers preserve visual fidelity, pixel reconstruction alone provides limited guidance for learning instruction-following dynamics that connect future prediction with robot control. To address this, we explore a semantic visual-action latent space for representation-centric world action modeling. Specifically, we train a representation visual-action tokenizer that maps visual inputs into aligned visual and latent action tokens. We then pretrain our WAM to jointly model future visual states and the latent actions that connect them under language instructions, followed by adaptation to real robot trajectories for closed-loop manipulation. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks show that RepWAM delivers strong performance across diverse manipulation settings, while ablations highlight the value of semantic visual-action tokenization over reconstruction-oriented alternatives. These results establish representation visual-action tokenization as a promising foundation for world action models and a step toward generalist robot policies. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/wdrink/RepWAM.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出RepWAM，一个基于表征性视觉-动作分词器（representation visual-action tokenizer）构建的表征中心世界动作模型（WAM）。现有WAM通常从预训练的视频生成模型中继承面向重建的视频分词器。尽管这些分词器保留了视觉保真度，但仅依赖像素重建来学习连接未来预测与机器人控制的指令跟随动态，所提供的指导十分有限。为解决这一问题，我们探索了一种用于表征中心世界动作建模的语义化视觉-动作潜在空间。具体来说，我们训练了一个表征性视觉-动作分词器，将视觉输入映射为对齐的视觉token和潜在动作token。随后，我们预训练WAM，使其在语言指令下联合建模未来视觉状态以及连接这些状态的潜在动作，并通过适配真实机器人轨迹实现闭环操作。在真实世界操作任务和仿真基准上的实验表明，RepWAM在多种操作场景下均展现出强大的性能，而消融实验则凸显了语义化视觉-动作分词化相对于面向重建替代方案的价值。这些结果确立了表征性视觉-动作分词化作为世界动作模型的有效基础，并朝着通用机器人策略迈出了重要一步。代码和权重将在https://github.com/wdrink/RepWAM上开源。

</details>


### [75] [Modality Forcing for Scalable Spatial Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13676)
*Bardienus Pieter Duisterhof,Deva Ramanan,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Justin Johnson,Keunhong Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Modality Forcing，一种简单可扩展的后训练方法，利用预训练文本到图像模型的空间先验，通过为每种模态分配独立噪声水平，实现图像和稀疏深度图的联合生成与条件生成，在单目深度估计上达到SOTA水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作利用T2I模型的空间先验进行深度预测，但需要密集深度数据且训练流程复杂。希望设计一种更简单、可扩展的方法，仅使用稀疏深度数据即可实现高质量的联合图像-深度生成与深度估计。

Method: 提出Modality Forcing：在单个DiT（扩散Transformer）上，为图像和深度两种模态分配不同的噪声水平，支持任意排列的条件生成和联合生成；使用每种模态独立的解码器，允许在稀疏真实世界深度数据上训练。

Result: 从零训练370M到3.3B参数的T2I模型，发现更大模型和更多图像数据可提升深度预测精度；最强模型与SOTA单目深度估计器相当，相比现有联合图像-深度生成模型降低AbsRel 57%。

Conclusion: 图像生成是一种可扩展的、用于空间感知的预训练目标，Modality Forcing有效继承T2I预训练的可扩展性，为稀疏深度条件下的联合生成与深度估计提供了简洁高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models contain rich spatial priors. Synthesizing photorealistic, cluttered scenes requires an understanding of geometry, including perspective and relative scale. Prior works adapt T2I models to leverage this prior for depth prediction, but they require dense depth data and involve complex recipes. We propose Modality Forcing, a simple, scalable post-training recipe for joint image-depth generation using a single DiT trained on sparse depth data. Modality Forcing enables conditional and joint generation of image and depth in any permutation by assigning separate noise levels per modality. Per-modality decoders let us train on sparse, real-world depth and achieve strong, generalizable depth prediction. We further show that Modality Forcing inherits the scalability of T2I pre-training: by training a set of T2I models from scratch (370M to 3.3B parameters), we find that larger models trained on more image data produce more accurate depth. Our strongest model is competitive with state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators and reduces AbsRel by 57% relative to existing joint image-depth generative models. These results provide strong evidence that image generation is a scalable pre-training objective for spatial perception. https://modality-forcing.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像（T2I）模型蕴含丰富的空间先验知识。合成逼真的杂乱场景需要理解几何信息，包括透视和相对尺度。现有工作利用T2I模型的空间先验进行深度预测，但需要密集深度数据且训练流程复杂。我们提出Modality Forcing，一种简单、可扩展的后训练方法，使用单个在稀疏深度数据上训练的DiT实现联合图像-深度生成。Modality Forcing通过为每种模态分配独立的噪声水平，支持图像和深度在任意排列下的条件生成和联合生成。每种模态独立的解码器使我们能够在稀疏的真实世界深度数据上训练，并获得强大且可泛化的深度预测能力。我们进一步证明Modality Forcing继承了T2I预训练的可扩展性：通过从零开始训练一组T2I模型（370M到3.3B参数），我们发现使用更多图像数据训练的更大模型能产生更精确的深度。我们最强的模型与最先进的单目深度估计器具有竞争力，相比现有联合图像-深度生成模型，AbsRel降低了57%。这些结果为"图像生成是一种可扩展的空间感知预训练目标"提供了有力证据。https://modality-forcing.github.io/

</details>


### [76] [InterleaveThinker: Reinforcing Agentic Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13679)
*Dian Zheng,Harry Lee,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Zoey Guo,Ray Zhang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterleaveThinker 是一个多智能体管道，通过规划器与批评器赋予现有图像生成器交叠生成（图文序列）能力，采用冷启动监督微调与单步强化学习（GRPO）实现高效指令修正，在交叠生成基准上达到与 Nano Banana 和 GPT-5 相当的性能，并显著提升基座模型在推理基准上的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像生成器在单图像生成与编辑上表现优异，但受限于架构无法实现交叠生成（图文交替序列），而交叠生成在视觉叙事、引导与具身操作等应用中至关重要；最新的统一多模态模型在此任务上表现有限，因此需要一种通用方案赋予已有生成器该能力。

Method: 提出 InterleaveThinker 多智能体管道：(1) 规划器代理组织图文输入序列，为图像生成器指定每步执行指令；(2) 批评器代理评估生成输出，识别偏离指令的样本并修正指令以重新生成。为实现该管道，构建 Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k 和 Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k 进行格式冷启动，再通过 GRPO 在 Interleave-Critic-RL-13k 上强化逐步指令修正能力。针对单次交叠生成轨迹可能涉及多次生成器调用导致优化困难的问题，提出准确率奖励和逐步奖励，使单步强化学习有效引导整个生成轨迹。

Result: InterleaveThinker 在多种图像生成器上均提升性能。在交叠生成基准上达到与 Nano Banana 和 GPT-5 相当的水平；在推理基准上显著增强基座模型，例如在 4-step FLUX.2-klein 上，WISE 和 RISE 指标均有大幅提升。

Conclusion: InterleaveThinker 作为首个多智能体交叠生成管道，能够有效赋予现有图像生成器交叠生成能力，不仅在该任务上达到前沿性能，还能提升基座模型在推理任务上的表现，验证了管道设计的通用性与有效性。

Original Abstract: Recent image generators have demonstrated impressive photorealism and instruction-following capabilities in single-image generation and editing. However, constrained by their architectures, they cannot achieve interleaved generation (text-image sequence), which has crucial applications in visual narratives, guidance, and embodied manipulation. Even the latest open-source Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) exhibit limited performance in this regard. In this paper, we introduce InterleaveThinker, the first multi-agent pipeline designed to endow any existing image generator with interleaved generation capabilities. Specifically, we employ a planner agent to organize the image-text input sequence, instructing the image generator on the required execution at each step. Subsequently, we introduce a critic agent to evaluate the generator's outputs, identify samples that deviate from the planned instructions, and refine the instructions for regeneration. To implement this pipeline, we construct the Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k and Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k to perform a format cold-start. Then we develop Interleave-Critic-RL-13k to reinforce the step-wise instruction correction capability within a generation trajectory using GRPO. Since a single interleaved generation trajectory may involve over 25 generator calls, optimizing the entire trajectory is computationally impractical. Therefore, we propose accuracy reward and step-wise reward, allowing single-step RL to effectively guide the entire generation trajectory. The results show that InterleaveThinker improves performance across various image generators. On interleaved generation benchmarks, it achieves performance comparable to Nano Banana and GPT-5. Surprisingly, it also significantly enhances the base model on reasoning-based benchmarks; for example, on 4-step FLUX.2-klein, we observe substantial gains on WISE and RISE.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的图像生成器在单图像生成与编辑中展现了令人印象深刻的逼真效果和指令跟随能力。然而，受限于其架构，它们无法实现交叠生成（图文序列），而交叠生成在视觉叙事、引导和具身操作中具有关键应用。即使是最新的开源统一多模态模型（UMMs）在这方面的表现也有限。在本文中，我们提出了 InterleaveThinker，这是首个旨在赋予任何现有图像生成器交叠生成能力的多智能体管道。具体来说，我们采用一个规划器代理来组织图文输入序列，指示图像生成器在每个步骤需要执行的操作。随后，我们引入一个批评器代理来评估生成器的输出，识别偏离规划指令的样本，并修正指令以重新生成。为实现这一管道，我们构建了 Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k 和 Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k 以执行格式冷启动。然后我们开发了 Interleave-Critic-RL-13k，利用 GRPO 在生成轨迹内强化逐步指令修正能力。由于单个交叠生成轨迹可能涉及超过 25 次生成器调用，优化整个轨迹在计算上不可行。因此，我们提出了准确率奖励和逐步奖励，使单步强化学习能够有效指导整个生成轨迹。结果表明，InterleaveThinker 在多种图像生成器上提升了性能。在交叠生成基准上，它达到了与 Nano Banana 和 GPT-5 相当的性能。令人惊讶的是，它在基于推理的基准上也显著增强了基座模型；例如，在 4-step FLUX.2-klein 上，我们在 WISE 和 RISE 上观察到了显著提升。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Fed-FBD: Federated Functional Block Diversification for Isolation, Privacy, and Surgical Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12679)
*Weijie Chen,Alan B. McMillan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-FBD提出了一种模块化联邦学习架构，通过将ResNet分解为六个功能块并维护贡献者标记的块仓库，在仅牺牲0.3%-3.1%精度的情况下实现了块级隔离、隐私保护和次秒级机器遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准联邦学习（如FedAvg）将每个客户端视为黑箱，缺乏隔离对抗性贡献者、审计客户端影响以及实现参与者被遗忘权的机制。

Method: 将ResNet骨干网络分解为六个功能块（stem、四个残差组和分类头），维护一个包含N个颜色变体的仓库，每个变体由独立跟踪和贡献者标记的块组装而成。

Result: 在MedMNIST-2D、PathMNIST和CIFAR-10上，Fed-FBD与FedAvg相比精度差距仅为0.3%-3.1%（IID）和0.8%-4.0%（Dirichlet alpha=1.0），且能将六种对抗攻击限制在被毒化客户端自己的块内，干净颜色变体的AUC漂移不超过±0.01。

Conclusion: Fed-FBD以可接受的精度代价，提供了FedAvg所不具备的块级隔离、设计内置隐私保护和高效机器遗忘能力。

Original Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw patient data, but standard approaches such as FedAvg treat each client as a black box and provide no mechanism for isolating an adversarial contributor, auditing per-client influence, or honoring a departed participant's right to be forgotten. We present Fed-FBD (Federated Functional Block Diversification), a modular federated architecture that decomposes a ResNet backbone into six functional blocks (the stem, four residual groups, and the classification head) and maintains a warehouse of N color variants, each assembled from independently tracked and contributor-stamped blocks. Fed-FBD provides three capabilities absent in FedAvg: (i) architecturally guaranteed block-level isolation, so that an adversarial or mislabelled client cannot contaminate the clean colous; (ii) privacy-by-design, where membership inference advantage is already indistinguishable from chance before any privacy mechanism is applied; and (iii) surgical machine unlearning of a departed participant's contribution at sub-second cost and without retraining. Experiments on six MedMNIST-2D datasets, PathMNIST at 224x224, and CIFAR-10 show that Fed-FBD trades a modest 0.3%-3.1% IID accuracy gap on the adequately sized datasets for these guarantees, remains within 0.8%-4.0% of FedAvg at Dirichlet alpha=1.0 on three of four datasets, and confines all six adversarial attacks we study to the poisoned client's own blocks with at most +/-0.01 AUC drift on the clean colors.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习（FL）能够在无需共享原始患者数据的情况下实现协作模型训练，但FedAvg等标准方法将每个客户端视为黑箱，无法隔离对抗性贡献者、审计每个客户端的影响，或尊重已退出参与者的被遗忘权。我们提出Fed-FBD（联邦功能块多样化），一种模块化联邦架构，它将ResNet骨干网络分解为六个功能块（stem、四个残差组和分类头），并维护一个包含N种颜色变体的仓库，每种变体由独立追踪且带有贡献者标记的块组装而成。Fed-FBD提供了FedAvg所不具备的三项能力：（i）架构上保证的块级隔离，使得对抗性或错误标注的客户端无法污染干净的颜色变体；（ii）设计内置隐私保护，在应用任何隐私机制之前，成员推断优势已与随机猜测不可区分；（iii）对已离开参与者的贡献进行外科手术式机器遗忘，耗时亚秒级且无需重新训练。在六个MedMNIST-2D数据集、224x224分辨率的PathMNIST以及CIFAR-10上的实验表明，在规模适中的数据集上，Fed-FBD为获得这些保障仅牺牲了0.3%-3.1%的IID精度差距；在四个数据集中的三个上，当Dirichlet alpha=1.0时，其性能与FedAvg的差距保持在0.8%-4.0%以内；并且，我们研究的所有六种对抗攻击均被限制在被毒化客户端自己的块内，干净颜色变体的AUC漂移最多不超过±0.01。

</details>


### [78] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将令牌级幻觉检测重新建模为快速变化检测问题，基于一阶马尔可夫模型和CUSUM框架，发现现有检测器在延迟-虚警权衡上远低于理论下界（1.3个令牌 vs 11-13个令牌），归因于学习到的得分仅实现了特征中信息散度的1/4.5。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的令牌级幻觉检测器以AUC等分类指标评估，忽略了流式监控场景中检测延迟这一关键指标。作者希望将幻觉检测问题纳入经典变化点检测理论框架，从而量化理论最优性能与实际检测器之间的差距。

Method: (1) 将幻觉检测建模为快速变化检测问题，采用一阶马尔可夫模型建模潜在忠实/幻觉状态；(2) 利用Lorden下界推导理论最优检测延迟；(3) 将因果循环标记器解释为具有学习增量的CUSUM检测器；(4) 基于Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理进行信息论分析。

Result: (1) 在虚警率0.01下，理论最优检测延迟下界约1.3个令牌；(2) 因果循环标记器检测延迟为11-13个令牌，线性逐令牌基线为31个令牌；(3) 优势主要来自更好的逐令牌得分而非时间累积；(4) 学习到的得分仅实现了特征中信息散度的1/4.5，该缺陷无法通过重新校准弥补；(5) 剩余差距为有限时域效应。

Conclusion: 分类指标（如AUC）掩盖了检测延迟结构，而序贯分析使其变得可测量。当前检测器在延迟性能上与理论最优存在一个数量级的差距，主要受限于学习得分的信息散度不足。

Original Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

中文摘要翻译: 令牌级幻觉检测器通常被作为分类器进行评估，即基于所有令牌计算AUC；然而，流式监控器的评判标准在于其反应时间：从幻觉发生到警报触发之间经过的令牌数量。本文将幻觉起始检测建模为一个快速变化检测问题。基于RAGTruth验证的一阶马尔可夫潜在忠实/幻觉状态模型，将任务嵌入经典变化点理论框架，并推导出Lorden下界：在虚警率为0.01时，检测延迟约为1.3个令牌。随后，我们证明因果循环标记器实际上充当了具有学习增量的CUSUM检测器；在匹配的虚警率下，其检测延迟为11-13个令牌，而线性逐令牌基线为31个令牌。通过受控分解，我们将这一优势主要归因于更好的逐令牌得分，而非时间累积效应。基于Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理，我们解释了剩余的数量级差距：学习到的得分仅实现了特征所携带信息散度的1/4.5——这一缺陷无法通过重新校准消除，而余量则源于有限时域效应。分类指标掩盖了这种延迟结构；序贯分析使其变得可测量。

</details>


### [79] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出玻尔兹曼注意力（Boltzmann attention），用伊辛模型对注意力模式建模，通过可学习的成对耦合参数显式建模位置间的交互，改进标准softmax注意力在序列建模中的表现，并探索了量子退火作为训练方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过单个查询-键相似度计算相关性，softmax归一化虽引入位置间竞争，但未显式参数化可学习的注意力决策间交互，难以直接建模注意力机制内部的协同或拮抗共注意力结构。

Method: 提出玻尔兹曼注意力（Boltzmann attention），将注意力模式建模为能量模型，具体为相互作用伊辛模型。在标准数据依赖的局部场基础上，增加可学习的成对耦合参数，使模型能够表示超越softmax或sigmoid注意力的位置间相关性。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务上，玻尔兹曼注意力一致优于标准softmax注意力，且优势随序列长度增加而更显著。四路消融实验证实改进来源于可学习的成对耦合。此外，绝热量子退火可在保持与精确玻尔兹曼计算相当性能的同时提供实用的训练方法。

Conclusion: 显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性增强，伊辛公式还为基于量子计算的采样策略开辟了自然路径。

Original Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

中文摘要翻译: 注意力机制是现代序列模型的核心，但标准注意力主要通过单个查询-键相似度计算相关性。尽管softmax归一化在位置间引入了竞争，标准的注意力层并未显式参数化注意力决策之间可学习的交互。这限制了其在注意力机制内部直接建模协同或拮抗共注意力结构的能力。我们提出玻尔兹曼注意力（Boltzmann attention），这是一种基于能量的泛化方法，其中注意力模式由相互作用的伊辛模型（Ising model）支配。该方法在通常的数据依赖局部场基础上增加了可学习的成对耦合，使模型能够表示超越softmax或sigmoid注意力所捕获的位置间相关性。在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配上的实验表明，在标准Transformer架构中，玻尔兹曼注意力一致优于标准softmax注意力，且优势随序列长度增加而更加显著。四路消融实验证实，性能提升来自可学习的成对耦合。这些结果表明，显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性增强。此外，伊辛公式为基于量子计算的采样策略开辟了自然路径：我们证明了绝热量子退火（diabatic quantum annealing）可提供实用的训练方法，同时保持与精确玻尔兹曼计算相当的性能。

</details>


### [80] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCal框架，通过层次化奖励分解与分布感知优化策略，解决强化学习路由中异构任务奖励信号模糊和优化偏差问题，提升路由性能与训练稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的LLM路由方法在处理异构任务时，存在两个关键问题：(1) 多个目标（如正确性、格式行为）被聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊和优化信号冲突；(2) 不同实例的奖励信号方差差异大，优化偏向简单样本而非信息量大的样本，引入优化偏差。

Method: 提出ReCal框架，包含两部分：(1) 层次化奖励分解机制，对组件进行优势估计；(2) 分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和逐数据集归一化校准优化变异性。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal在路由性能和训练稳定性上持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: ReCal通过奖励校准有效解决了RL-based LLM路由中的优化偏差和信用分配问题，为异构任务下的动态模型与策略选择提供了更优方案。

Original Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）路由已成为一种有效范式，通过动态模型和推理策略选择来利用多个LLM的互补优势。最近的基于强化学习（RL）的路由方法通过从交互反馈中优化路由策略，进一步提升了路由质量。然而，它们仍难以在难度各异的异构任务中提供信息丰富且可比较的学习信号。在实践中，多个目标（如正确性、格式行为）被聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊和优化信号冲突。此外，不同实例间的奖励信号表现出显著变异性，某些实例产生更高或更可变的奖励，从而引入有利于简单样本而非信息性样本的优化偏差。为解决这些问题，我们提出\textbf{ReCal}，一个用于基于RL的LLM路由的\textbf{\underline{奖励校准}}框架。我们首先引入一种层次化奖励分解机制，结合组件级优势估计。进一步提出一种分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和逐数据集归一化来校准优化变异性。在七个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal在路由性能和训练稳定性上持续优于基线方法。代码已开源：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal。

</details>


### [81] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出T2SP方法，将时间序列表示为结构化符号程序，使LLM无需微调即可有效推理时间序列，在编辑、描述和问答三个任务上优于原始字符串表示。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将原始数值序列直接输入LLM或微调预训练模型，导致模态不匹配、长序列性能下降且计算开销大。需要一种将时序结构提取从模型转移到表示本身的桥梁方法。

Method: T2SP（时间序列到结构化程序表示）是一种确定性、无需训练的方法，将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，用结构化符号程序格式表达，使其与LLM原生训练的文法和代码模态对齐。

Result: 在编辑、描述和问答三个推理任务上，T2SP相比原始字符串表示持续提升性能、减少推理时间、降低失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP为时间序列与LLM之间提供了有效的接口，使现成LLM无需微调即可利用其原有推理能力进行时间序列理解。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）展现了强大的推理和指令遵循能力，使其成为时间序列分析的潜在强大工具。然而，时间序列超出了其原生的文本模态，这引发了一个根本性问题：如何表示时间序列才能使LLM有效地对其进行推理？现有工作通常将原始数值序列序列化，或在时间序列数据上微调预训练的LLM。这些方法将提取时序结构的负担直接放在LLM上，造成了模态不匹配，这通常会导致长序列上的性能下降并引入大量计算开销。在这项工作中，我们提出了时间序列到结构化程序表示（T2SP），一种确定性的、无需训练的方法，将时间序列表示为结构化的符号程序。T2SP将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，以与LLM原生训练的文法和代码模态对齐的程序友好格式进行表达。通过将时序结构提取从模型转移到表示本身，T2SP使现成的LLM能够利用其现有的推理能力进行时间序列理解。我们在三个推理任务——编辑、描述和问答——上评估了T2SP，与原始字符串表示相比，它持续提升了性能，减少了推理时间，并降低了失败率。我们的结果表明，T2SP为时间序列和LLM之间提供了有效的接口。

</details>


### [82] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），通过基于查询点与支持点之间平方距离的方法，解决了Christoffel函数在高维数据中因矩阵求逆导致的维度灾难问题，在ADBench基准上超越了14个基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统Christoffel函数方法在异常检测中具有坚实的数学基础且计算节俭，但其需要求逆的矩阵规模随数据维度呈指数增长，导致该方法在中高维数据集上不可行，亟需解决其维度可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离构建单变量形式的Christoffel函数，避免了原始方法中高维矩阵求逆的计算瓶颈，同时保留了关键的支撑二分性和精确支撑形状捕获等理论性质。

Result: 在ADBench基准上的大量实验表明，UCF在平均精度（Average Precision）指标上持续优于14个最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过解决Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，本文为异常检测工具箱增添了一种鲁棒、有理论基础且普遍适用的方法。

Original Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

中文摘要翻译: 异常检测在欺诈检测、网络入侵和系统故障诊断等领域识别异常模式方面发挥着关键作用。近年来，基于Christoffel函数（源于多项式优化）的方法因其坚实的数学基础和计算节俭性，已成为深度学习的有前景替代方案。然而，其实际应用受到需要求逆一个规模随数据维度呈指数增长的矩阵的限制，这使得该方法即使对中等维度的数据集也难以处理。本文解决了基于Christoffel函数的异常检测的维度限制问题，同时保留了其关键理论性质，即开关支撑二分性行为和精确支撑形状捕获。我们引入UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），它基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离。在ADBench基准上的大量实验表明，UCF在平均精度指标上持续优于14个最先进的基线方法。通过解决Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，本文为异常检测工具箱增添了一种鲁棒、有理论基础且普遍适用的方法。

</details>


### [83] [Extracting Governing Equations from Latent Dynamics via Multi-View Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13260)
*Paolo Muratore,Mackenzie Weygandt Mathis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DYSCO 是一种多视图时序对比学习算法，能从高维噪声观测中联合恢复潜在轨迹及其动力学方程，并支持符号形式的方程恢复，理论上保证了仿射变换下的可辨识性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从含噪声的高维观测数据中识别潜在动力系统是表示学习、系统辨识和科学发现的核心问题，现有方法在非线性噪声观测场景下缺乏理论保证和实用效果。

Method: 提出多视图时序对比学习算法 DYSCO，利用同一底层过程的多个独立噪声视图来分离信号与噪声，将动力学参数化为结构化函数基，支持仿射规范下的符号方程恢复。

Result: 在混沌、振荡、亚稳态等多种动力学系统中，DYSCO 在高斯和泊松观测噪声下均能准确恢复潜在轨迹和流场，后者尤其适用于神经记录场景。

Conclusion: DYSCO 将先前的可辨识性结果扩展到含噪非线性观测的现实场景，为从噪声高维数据中发现潜在动力系统提供了理论保证和有效方法。

Original Abstract: Identifying latent dynamical systems from noisy, high-dimensional measurements is a central problem at the intersection of representation learning, system identification, and scientific discovery. We present DYSCO, a multi-view temporal contrastive learning algorithm that jointly recovers latent trajectories and the governing dynamics from such observations, by leveraging multiple independent noisy views of the same underlying process to disentangle signal from noise. By parameterizing the dynamics in a structured functional basis, our framework further enables symbolic recovery of the governing equations within an affine gauge. We offer theoretical guarantees for strong identification up to an affine indeterminacy, extending prior identifiability results to the realistic setting of noisy nonlinear observations. Empirically, we demonstrate accurate recovery of both latent trajectories and flow fields across a diverse set of dynamical regimes (e.g., chaotic, oscillatory, and metastable) under both Gaussian and Poisson observation noise, the latter being particularly relevant for neural recordings.

中文摘要翻译: 从含噪声的高维测量数据中识别潜在动力系统，是表示学习、系统辨识和科学发现交叉领域的核心问题。我们提出 DYSCO，一种多视图时序对比学习算法，通过利用同一底层过程的多个独立噪声视图来分离信号与噪声，从而从这类观测中联合恢复潜在轨迹和控制动力学。通过将动力学参数化为结构化函数基，我们的框架进一步支持在仿射规范下对控制方程进行符号恢复。我们提供了仿射不确定性下强可辨识性的理论保证，将先前的可辨识性结果扩展到了含噪非线性观测的现实场景。在实验上，我们在高斯和泊松观测噪声下（后者尤其适用于神经记录），在多种动力学系统（如混沌、振荡和亚稳态）中展示了潜在轨迹和流场的准确恢复。

</details>


### [84] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为推测性回滚修正（SRC）的分支级模仿学习框架，通过固定视界分支审查和回滚机制，在交互式网页代理训练中平衡早期错误纠正与避免过度依赖专家策略的问题，在WebArena-Infinity上收集了977条通过验证的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在交互式网页代理的模仿学习中，专家干预的时机存在两难困境：延迟干预会导致早期错误累积使页面状态不可恢复；过早或过度干预则使代理过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一刚性轨迹的局部最优。现有方法要么在每个访问状态都请求教师标签，要么仅在完整轨迹完成后才进行纠正，均无法有效解决这一权衡问题。

Method: 提出推测性回滚修正（SRC）框架，是一种针对可重置代理环境的分支级模仿框架。具体包括：(1) 固定视界分支审查：学生先执行一段短推测性片段，教师仅在局部进度停滞时才定位首个有害偏差；(2) 回滚保留有用前缀；(3) 成功展开通过硬验证器过滤并存入轻量级质量多样性存档；(4) 对局部化纠正和通过验证的轨迹进行下一动作监督微调。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条通过验证器的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例。固定视界审查在恢复与查询的权衡上优于步级审查，同时保留了通过验证的解决方案变体。

Conclusion: SRC通过分支级模仿学习和回滚机制，有效解决了交互式代理训练中专家干预时机的两难问题，在减少专家查询次数的同时提高了错误恢复能力，并保持了解决方案的多样性。

Original Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

中文摘要翻译: 通过从专家轨迹进行模仿学习来训练交互式网页代理已成为一种高效的方法。然而，确定专家干预的最佳时机在此背景下是一个关键挑战。延迟干预通常导致早期错误累积，将页面状态推入不可恢复的境地。反之，过早或过度干预会导致代理过度依赖专家策略，使模型陷入以单一刚性轨迹为特征的局部最优。我们提出推测性回滚修正（SRC），一种针对可重置代理环境的分支级模仿框架。SRC并非在每个访问状态都请求教师标签，也非仅在完整轨迹完成后才进行纠正，而是采用固定视界分支审查：学生先执行一段短推测性片段，然后教师进行审查，且教师仅在局部进度停滞时才定位第一个有害偏差。回滚机制保留有用的前缀，而成功的展开通过硬验证器过滤并存入轻量级质量多样性存档。所得数据支持在局部化纠正和通过验证的轨迹上进行下一动作监督微调。在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条通过验证器的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例；固定视界审查在恢复与查询的权衡上优于步级审查，同时保留了通过验证的解决方案变体。代码可在https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent获取。

</details>


### [85] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于状态预测性维护（PdM）方法，假设监测部件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，仅选取最近观测值并转换为表格格式，使用AutoML进行机器学习分类，在Scania Component X数据集上相比SOTA方法降低了成本并简化了建模流程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卡车车队基于状态的预测性维护实施面临数据量大、通过传感器数据检测故障复杂、以及实现成本效益权衡困难等挑战，因此需要一种更高效、低成本且易于实施的PdM方法。

Method: 假设监测部件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，仅选取时间序列中最新的观测值，将其转换为表格格式，然后使用针对表格数据设计的机器学习模型（通过AutoML）进行分类。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，所提方法相比当前最先进方法降低了成本，同时通过AutoML简化了建模过程。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于状态的预测性维护方法在降低成本方面优于现有SOTA方法，且AutoML的使用使建模过程更加简化，具有实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

中文摘要翻译: 基于状态的卡车车队预测性维护（PdM）近年来发展迅速。该维护策略旨在通过监测车辆的健康状态并根据其状况采取主动措施，以最小化非计划停机时间并降低成本。然而，由于卡车产生的大量数据、通过传感器数据检测故障的固有复杂性以及在解决方案实施中寻找成本效益权衡的困难，基于状态的PdM系统的实施面临挑战。在本文中，我们定义并验证了一种基于状态的PdM方法，该方法基于以下假设：监测部件的磨损状态可以表示为单调非递减的时间序列。该方法仅从时间序列中选择最近的观测值，并将其转换为表格格式，以便使用针对表格数据设计的机器学习（ML）模型进行分类。我们的结果表明，与当前最先进（SOTA）方法相比，所提出的方法在Scania Component X数据集上降低了成本，同时通过AutoML简化了建模过程。

</details>


### [86] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 动态PTQ方法通过识别残差流中相位式的大值激活波动，仅对敏感层使用8-bit激活精度，其余保持4-bit，在W4A4KV4量化下提升LLM推理性能与吞吐量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PTQ方法主要通过变换式平滑（如正交旋转、仿射缩放）缓解大值激活问题，但忽略了残差流的跨层动态特性——大值激活以相位模式在网络深度中出现和消失，导致静态变换无法完全解决跨层残差变化引起的动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 提出DynamicPTQ动态后训练量化策略：基于跳跃比和历史特征信噪比识别残差流动态中量化敏感的层，仅对这些层分配8-bit激活精度，而权重、KV缓存和其他激活保持4-bit精度；可直接集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等强PTQ基线中。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的实验表明，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化下持续提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，并实现1.05~1.07倍的吞吐量提升，内存开销适中。

Conclusion: 提出了一种实用的低比特LLM推理路径：通过动态感知残差流相位变化，以极小的精度开销（仅少数层用8-bit）即可显著改善全4-bit量化下的模型性能，验证了混合精度策略在稳健低比特推理中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR.
