### [200] [Chirp Parameter Optimization and Distributed Detection for Cooperative RSMA-AFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12823)
*Qingyu Li,Guanghui Liu,Yusha Liu,Fuchen Xu,Chengxiang Liu,Hongjun Liu,Liaoyuan Zeng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对仿射频分复用(AFDM)系统因信号色散特性难以直接采用传统时频多址方案的问题，引入协作速率分割多址接入(RSMA)，通过优化AFDM啁啾参数降低用户间等效信道相关性并最大化分集增益，提出了两种基于期望传播(EP)的分布式协作检测方案以提升系统可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AFDM系统具有出色的多普勒鲁棒性和双选择性信道表征能力，但由于其信号色散特性，传统时频多址方案难以直接应用于AFDM系统，需要设计适配的多址接入方案。

Method: 1) 引入协作RSMA到AFDM系统中；2) 通过理论分析证明最小化用户信道列空间重叠可提升系统性能；3) 设计啁啾参数优化方案以降低多用户干扰并最大化分集增益；4) 提出两种基于期望传播(EP)的分布式协作检测方案：基于决策融合的方法（最大比合并）和基于置信共识EP的检测方案（迭代交换并融合公共流的一阶和二阶统计量）。

Result: 理论分析表明，最小化用户间信道列空间重叠能有效提升系统性能；提出的啁啾参数优化方案可降低多用户干扰并最大化分集增益；两种基于EP的分布式协作检测方案中，置信共识EP检测方案通过迭代交换公共流统计量使信念逐渐收敛到一致的全局决策，显著提升了整体可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的协作RSMA-AFDM系统结合啁啾参数优化与EP分布式检测，有效解决了AFDM信号色散带来的多址接入难题，提升了系统在多用户场景下的性能与可靠性。

Original Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) exhibits excellent Doppler robustness and the ability to characterize doubly selective channels. However, its signal dispersion characteristics make it challenging to directly adopt traditional time-frequency multiple access schemes. To address this issue, we introduce cooperative rate splitting multiple access (RSMA) for AFDM systems. The flexible configuration of AFDM chirp parameters can reduce the correlation between users' equivalent channels, which decreases the interference from RSMA private streams. We conduct a theoretical analysis of the cooperative RSMA-AFDM system and demonstrate that minimizing the overlap in the channel column spaces among users can effectively enhance the system performance. Guided by this analysis, we design a chirp parameter optimization scheme that reduces multi-user interference and maximizes diversity gain. To fully exploit the diversity gain brought by the proposed chirp parameter optimization, two expectation propagation (EP)-based distributed cooperative detection schemes are proposed. First, a decision-fusion-based method is developed, where local information and cooperative information are fused by maximum ratio combining, achieving a globally consistent estimate of the common stream. Second, we develop a belief-consensus EP-based detection scheme. In each iteration, user nodes exchange and fuse the first- and second-order statistics of the common stream, and the resulting beliefs gradually converge to a consistent global decision, which significantly improves the overall reliability.

中文摘要翻译: 仿射频分复用(AFDM)技术展现出出色的多普勒鲁棒性和表征双选择性信道的能力。然而，其信号色散特性使得直接采用传统时频多址方案面临挑战。针对这一问题，我们将协作速率分割多址接入(RSMA)引入AFDM系统。AFDM啁啾参数的灵活配置可以降低用户间等效信道的相关性，从而减少RSMA私有流的干扰。我们对协作RSMA-AFDM系统进行了理论分析，证明最小化用户间信道列空间的重叠可以有效提升系统性能。基于该分析，我们设计了一种啁啾参数优化方案，以降低多用户干扰并最大化分集增益。为充分利用所提啁啾参数优化带来的分集增益，提出了两种基于期望传播(EP)的分布式协作检测方案。首先，开发了一种基于决策融合的方法，通过最大比合并融合本地信息与协作信息，实现对公共流的全局一致估计。其次，我们提出了一种基于置信共识EP的检测方案。在每次迭代中，用户节点交换并融合公共流的一阶和二阶统计量，生成的信念逐渐收敛到一致的全局决策，从而显著提高整体可靠性。

</details>


### [201] [Sub-array Selection Optimization for Joint Self-Interference and Multi-User Interference Suppression in FD mMIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12831)
*Yuanzhe Gong,Yuanxing Zhang,Tho Le-Ngoc*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对全双工大规模MIMO系统，提出了一种联合天线子阵列选择（SAS）和基于角度扰动的零陷生成（APN）的波束赋形优化方案，用于同时抑制自干扰和多用户干扰。通过在8×8 Tx-8×8 Rx全双工阵列原型上进行实测信道测量，开发了基于粒子群优化（PSO）的算法来联合选择最优子阵列索引和扰动导向角。实验结果表明，该方法实现了显著的干扰抑制性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全双工大规模MIMO系统中，自干扰（SI）和多用户干扰（MUI）的抑制是关键挑战。现有研究缺乏对实际空中SI信道在不同子阵列配置下变化特性的充分测量与理解，需要探索如何利用SI信道的选择性来提升波束级隔离度。

Method: 首先通过8×8 Tx-8×8 Rx全双工阵列原型进行全面的空中SI信道测量，揭示不同空间位置子阵列间的信道变化及可重构特性。然后提出基于粒子群优化（PSO）的算法，联合确定最优子阵列索引和扰动导向角，有效零化潜在干扰。

Result: 基于实测SI信道的实验评估表明：对于1×2和1×4子阵列，残余Tx-Rx波束级SI抑制分别提升29.2 dB和26.6 dB，最差情况改善超过30.7 dB。联合SAS和APN优化方案在1×2和1×4子阵列下分别实现平均波束级隔离度85.2 dB和83.3 dB。应用基带预编码器后，所有子阵列配置的平均MUI抑制优于-181.3 dB。

Conclusion: 该优化方案能够成功将干扰降低至噪声基底水平，从而保证可靠的全双工大规模MIMO运行，验证了所提算法在实际系统中的可行性与有效性。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes a beamforming optimization scheme with joint antenna sub-array selection (SAS) and angular perturbation-based nulling (APN) for full-duplex (FD) massive multiple-input multiple-output (mMIMO) systems, to simultaneously suppress self-interference (SI) and multi-user interference (MUI). A comprehensive over-the-air SI channel measurement campaign, conducted with an 8x8Tx-8x8Rx FD array prototype, reveals significant variations across sub-arrays at different spatial locations, as well as reconfigurable characteristics of the SI channel under diverse Tx and Rx sub-array configurations. To exploit the selective SI channels, a particle swarm optimization (PSO)-based algorithm is developed to jointly determine optimal sub-array indices and perturbed steering angles, thereby effectively nullifying potential interference. Selecting sub-arrays with inherently lower SI channels notably enhances the beam-level isolation, while the added selection flexibility among comparable SI channels ensures more uniform SI suppression across diverse DL/UL locations and significantly improves worst-case isolation. Experimental evaluation based on the measured SI channel demonstrates that the proposed SAS technique achieves residual Tx-Rx beam-level SI suppression improvements of 29.2 dB and 26.6 dB for the sample 1x2 and 1x4 sub-arrays, respectively. A worst-case improvement greater than 30.7 dB is observed. Overall, the joint SAS and APN optimization scheme achieves average beam-level isolation of 85.2 dB and 83.3 dB with the 1x2 and 1x4 sub-arrays, respectively. With the application of a baseband precoder, all tested sub-array configurations achieve average MUI suppression better than -181.3 dB. These results confirm the potential of the proposed optimization algorithm to successfully reduce interference to the noise floor, thereby guaranteeing reliable FD mMIMO operation.

中文摘要翻译: 本文针对全双工（FD）大规模多输入多输出（mMIMO）系统，提出了一种联合天线子阵列选择（SAS）和基于角度扰动的零陷生成（APN）的波束赋形优化方案，用于同时抑制自干扰（SI）和多用户干扰（MUI）。通过使用8×8发射-8×8接收全双工阵列原型进行的全面空中SI信道测量，揭示了不同空间位置子阵列之间显著的SI信道变化，以及在不同发射和接收子阵列配置下SI信道的可重构特性。为了利用具有选择性的SI信道，开发了一种基于粒子群优化（PSO）的算法，用于联合确定最优子阵列索引和扰动导向角，从而有效零化潜在干扰。选择固有SI信道较低的子阵列可以显著增强波束级隔离度，而在具有可比SI信道的子阵列间增加的选择灵活性，确保了在不同下行/上行位置上更均匀的SI抑制，并显著改善了最差情况下的隔离度。基于实测SI信道的实验评估表明，所提出的SAS技术对于1×2和1×4子阵列的样本，残余发射-接收波束级SI抑制分别提升了29.2 dB和26.6 dB。观察到最差情况改善超过30.7 dB。总体而言，联合SAS和APN优化方案在1×2和1×4子阵列下分别实现了85.2 dB和83.3 dB的平均波束级隔离度。应用基带预编码器后，所有测试的子阵列配置均实现了优于-181.3 dB的平均MUI抑制。这些结果证实了所提出的优化算法能够成功将干扰降低至噪声基底水平，从而保证可靠的全双工大规模MIMO运行。

</details>


### [202] [Active Perception for Radio Map Reconstruction in Uncharted 3D Air-Ground Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12844)
*Wenlihan Lu,Miaowen Wen,Shijian Gao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-URAM，一种用于低空网络系统中三维无线电地图主动构建的闭环感知框架。它通过两阶段离线训练——贝叶斯UNet恢复地图并估计不确定性，以及基于PPO的Transformer路径规划策略——在稀疏测量和能量约束下实现高效主动建图，重建误差降低超50%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统地面无线电地图可通过路测生成，但空-地环境需要部署无人机，面临两个关键挑战：一是稀疏的无线电测量和不完整几何观测导致重建困难；二是巨大的3D动作空间和高频谱扫描仪能耗带来的严格功率约束，使得信息性探索变得困难。

Method: 提出3D-URAM闭环主动感知框架，将建图过程解耦为两个离线训练阶段：阶段I使用贝叶斯UNet从稀疏测量和部分几何中恢复无线电地图，同时提供校准的预测不确定性；阶段II构建动态概率路图，并设计基于Transformer的航点选择策略，通过近端策略优化（PPO）进行训练，在旅行预算约束下最大化长时域的不确定性降低。

Result: 相比代表性基线方法，3D-URAM将重建误差降低了超过50%。在300m×200m×100m空间内的真实世界外场测试也验证了主动无线电地图重建的潜力。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过将地图重建与不确定性驱动的主动探索相结合，可以在稀疏测量和能量受限的条件下实现高效的三维无线电地图构建，为低空网络系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Radio maps provide the essential foundation for low altitude networking systems. Unlike terrestrial radio maps that are typically generated via drive test measurements, mapping the air-ground environment requires the deployment of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). This shift introduces two formidable challenges in uncharted 3D scenarios. First, sparse radio measurements and incomplete geometric observations hinder accurate reconstruction. Second, the large 3D action space and strict power constraints from high spectrum scanner energy consumption make informative exploration difficult. To address these issues, this paper proposes 3D uncertainty aware radio active mapping (3D-URAM), a closed loop active perception framework that decouples the mapping process into two offline trained stages. In Stage I, a Bayesian UNet is developed to recover radio maps from sparse measurements and partial geometry while providing calibrated predictive uncertainty. In Stage II, a dynamic probabilistic roadmap and a transformer based waypoint selection policy trained via proximal policy optimization maximize long horizon uncertainty reduction under travel budgets. Experimental results demonstrate that 3D-URAM reduces reconstruction error by over 50% compared to representative baselines. Real-world field tests within a 300mx200mx100m space also validate the potential of active radio map reconstruction.

中文摘要翻译: 无线电地图为低空组网系统提供了基础支撑。与通常通过路测生成的地面无线电地图不同，空-地环境的测绘需要部署无人机。这一转变在未知的三维场景中引入了两个严峻挑战：首先，稀疏的无线电测量和不完整的几何观测阻碍了精确重建；其次，巨大的三维动作空间和高频谱扫描仪能耗带来的严格功率约束使得信息性探索变得困难。为解决这些问题，本文提出了三维不确定性感知无线电主动建图（3D-URAM），一种将建图过程解耦为两个离线训练阶段的闭环主动感知框架。在第一阶段，开发了贝叶斯UNet，用于从稀疏测量和部分几何信息中恢复无线电地图，同时提供校准的预测不确定性。在第二阶段，构建了动态概率路图，并设计了基于Transformer的航点选择策略，通过近端策略优化进行训练，在旅行预算约束下最大化长时域的不确定性降低。实验结果表明，与代表性基线方法相比，3D-URAM将重建误差降低了超过50%。在300m×200m×100m空间内的真实世界外场测试也验证了主动无线电地图重建的潜力。

</details>


### [203] [Rotatable Antenna-Enabled Near-Field Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12870)
*Zequan Wang,Liang Yin,Yitong Liu,Hongwen Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出利用可旋转天线（RA）在近场通信与感知中开发新的方向域空间自由度，通过联合优化接收波束赋形器、数字/模拟波束赋形器及RA视轴方向，实现混合波束赋形下的近场集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统固定天线难以充分利用近场通信与感知中的空间自由度，而可旋转天线通过元件级的天线旋转提供了新的方向域自由度，有望补偿射频链路数量受限带来的性能损失，同时提升近场感知的离轴精度和杂波抑制能力。

Method: 建立了考虑方向相关天线增益的球面波信道模型，构建加权通信-感知效用最大化问题，提出交替优化算法，结合分数规划、黎曼优化和基于球冠Frank-Wolfe的视轴更新方法进行求解，并推导了闭式根克拉美-罗界（RCRB）表达式。

Result: 仿真结果表明所提算法收敛且有效；RA增强的混合波束赋形设计在某些场景下可匹敌甚至超越全数字固定天线基准；RA旋转可提升离轴感知精度、增强距离域聚焦能力并抑制近场同角度杂波。

Conclusion: 可旋转天线通过方向域自由度弥补了有限射频链路的不足，在近场ISAC系统中展现出显著优势，能够有效提升通信与感知性能。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose leveraging rotatable antennas (RAs) to enhance near-field communication and sensing by exploiting a new orientation-domain spatial degree-of-freedom (DoF) provided by element-wise antenna rotation. Specifically, we investigate an RA-enabled near-field integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system with sub-connected hybrid beamforming, where each transmit RA can independently adjust its boresight direction under a practical rotation constraint. A spherical-wave channel model incorporating orientation-dependent antenna gains is established to characterize multi-user communication and target sensing in the presence of clutters. Based on this model, a weighted communication-sensing utility maximization problem is formulated by jointly optimizing the receive beamformer, digital beamformer, analog beamformer, and RA boresight directions. To solve the resulting non-convex problem, an alternating optimization algorithm is developed by combining fractional programming, Riemannian optimization, and a spherical-cap Frank--Wolfe-based boresight update. To further understand the impact of RA rotation on near-field sensing, we derive a closed-form root Cramer--Rao bound (RCRB) expression. Simulation results demonstrate the convergence and effectiveness of the proposed algorithm. It is shown that the RA-enabled hybrid design can match or even outperform the fully-digital FPA benchmark in some regimes, indicating that the orientation-domain DoF introduced by element-wise rotation can compensate for limited RF chains. The RCRB and beampattern results further show that RA rotation improves off-broadside sensing accuracy, enhances range-domain focusing, and suppresses same-angle clutters in the near field.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出利用可旋转天线（RA）通过元件级天线旋转提供的新方向域空间自由度来增强近场通信与感知。具体而言，我们研究了一个采用子连接混合波束赋形的RA增强近场集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，其中每个发射RA可以在实际旋转约束下独立调整其视轴方向。建立了考虑方向相关天线增益的球面波信道模型，以表征存在杂波情况下的多用户通信与目标感知。基于该模型，通过联合优化接收波束赋形器、数字波束赋形器、模拟波束赋形器和RA视轴方向，构建了一个加权通信-感知效用最大化问题。为求解所得到的非凸问题，发展了一种结合分数规划、黎曼优化和基于球冠Frank-Wolfe视轴更新的交替优化算法。为进一步理解RA旋转对近场感知的影响，我们推导了闭式根克拉美-罗界（RCRB）表达式。仿真结果证明了所提算法的收敛性和有效性。结果表明，RA增强的混合设计在某些场景下可以匹敌甚至超越全数字固定天线基准，说明元件级旋转引入的方向域自由度可以补偿有限的射频链路。RCRB和波束方向图结果进一步表明，RA旋转能够提升离轴感知精度、增强距离域聚焦能力，并在近场中抑制同角度杂波。

</details>


### [204] [LGVSC: A Large-Model-Driven Generative Video Semantic Communication Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12899)
*Yu Ma,Hang Yin,Li Qiao,Shuo Sun,Zhen Gao,Yin Xu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种大模型驱动的生成式视频语义通信框架（LGVSC），通过解耦编解码器、显式中间语义表示、基于概率的语义相似度评分（PSSS）和语义引导的关键帧提取模块，在极低带宽（信道带宽比10^-4~10^-3）下实现高效视频语义传输，并保持可解释性与零样本泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 万物互联时代视频传输需求巨大，语义通信有望在传输效率与质量间取得平衡，但现有端到端系统存在黑盒行为、缺乏可解释性，且缺乏适用于复杂模态的细粒度语义评估指标。

Method: 1) 解耦编码器与解码器，显式暴露中间语义表示以保持可解释性；2) 提出概率语义相似度评分（PSSS）量化连续范围内的语义相似度；3) 基于PSSS设计多模态大模型驱动的语义引导关键帧提取模块，增强细粒度语义一致性；4) 在接收端设计生成式大模型驱动的动态语义自适应解码器，支持任意长度视频。

Result: LGVSC在信道带宽比低至10^-4到10^-3的数量级上显著优于传统方案，并在下游任务上保持强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: LGVSC框架通过大模型驱动的生成式语义通信，在极低带宽条件下实现了高效、可解释的视频语义传输，验证了语义通信在万物互联中的巨大潜力。

Original Abstract: Driven by the massive video transmission requirements in the Internet of Everything, semantic communication holds great promise for striking a balance between transmission efficiency and quality. This paper introduces a large-model-driven generative video semantic communication (LGVSC) framework, enabling efficient video semantic transmission under extremely low bandwidth conditions. First, by decoupling the encoder and decoder as well as exposing explicit intermediate semantic representations, LGVSC maintains interpretability, avoiding the black-box behavior commonly observed in end-to-end systems. Next, we introduce a new metric, i.e., the probability-based semantic similarity score (PSSS), which quantifies semantic similarity for complex modalities within a continuous range, allowing for more precise evaluation of semantic content. Building on PSSS, we propose a semantic-guided keyframe extraction module driven by a multimodal large model. This module can enhance fine-grained semantic consistency during keyframe selection at the transmitter, optimizing transmission bandwidth without compromising semantic fidelity. Additionally, we design a generative large-model-driven dynamic semantic-adaptive decoder at the receiver, which can adapt to videos of arbitrary lengths. Simulation results demonstrate that LGVSC significantly outperforms traditional schemes, achieving a channel bandwidth ratio on the order of 10^-4 to 10^-3, while maintaining strong zero-shot generalization across downstream tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 受万物互联中海量视频传输需求的驱动，语义通信在平衡传输效率与质量方面展现出巨大潜力。本文提出了一种大模型驱动的生成式视频语义通信（LGVSC）框架，能够在极低带宽条件下实现高效的视频语义传输。首先，通过解耦编码器与解码器并显式暴露中间语义表示，LGVSC保持了可解释性，避免了端到端系统中常见的黑盒行为。其次，我们引入了一种新的度量指标——基于概率的语义相似度评分（PSSS），该指标可在连续范围内量化复杂模态的语义相似度，从而实现对语义内容更精确的评估。基于PSSS，我们提出了一个由多模态大模型驱动的语义引导关键帧提取模块，该模块能够在发送端增强关键帧选择过程中的细粒度语义一致性，在不牺牲语义保真度的前提下优化传输带宽。此外，我们在接收端设计了一个由生成式大模型驱动的动态语义自适应解码器，能够适应任意长度的视频。仿真结果表明，LGVSC显著优于传统方案，实现了信道带宽比在10^-4到10^-3数量级的性能，同时在下游任务中保持强大的零样本泛化能力。

</details>


### [205] [Simulating Torsional Vibrations of Faulty Bevel Gears Using the Polygonal Contact Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12967)
*Milla Vehviläinen,Aleksanteri Hämäläinen,Pekka Rahkola,Mikko Savolainen,Janne Keränen,Jari Halme,Jenni Pippuri-Mäkeläinen,Kari Tammi,Anouar Belahcen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于先进多边形接触方法的多体仿真技术，用于模拟方位推力器中健康与故障齿轮的扭转振动，生成的仿真信号在时域和频域上与实测数据高度一致，为数据驱动的预测性维护提供了坚实基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 机电应用中齿轮的状态监测高度依赖高质量故障数据，但故障数据往往稀缺。为解决数据不足的问题，作者提出了一种能够模拟任意故障几何形状的多体仿真方法。

Method: 采用一种先进的多边形接触方法进行多体仿真，在实验方位推力器测试台架上复现扭转振动信号，支持模拟健康齿轮和具有任意故障几何形状的故障齿轮。

Result: 仿真信号与实测信号在时域和频域上均高度吻合：时域上平均扭矩水平和周期性波动匹配良好；频域上准确再现了预期的故障频率及其边带，且较大故障产生更高幅值。仿真倾向于高估峰值幅值并低估外部噪声，但整体结果与实测高度可比且符合物理预期。

Conclusion: 该仿真方法为增强数据驱动的状态监测方法（尤其基于机器学习和深度学习的方法）提供了坚实基础。

Original Abstract: Gears are an integral component of electromechanical applications, but accurate condition monitoring methods, including data-driven predictive maintenance, are strongly dependent on high-quality data, especially from faulty components. To address the scarcity of data, we proposed a multibody simulation using an advanced polygonal contact method to replicate torsional vibrations from an experimental azimuth thruster test rig. The key novelty is the ability to simulate both healthy and faulty gears with arbitrary fault geometries. The simulated signals closely matched the measurements in both time and frequency domains. In the time domain, average torque levels and periodic fluctuations aligned well, although measured signals exhibited higher peak-to-peak amplitudes and greater noise, particularly in healthy conditions at lower rotational speeds. In the frequency domain, the simulations accurately reproduced expected fault frequencies and corresponding sidebands, with larger faults producing higher amplitudes. While the simulations tended to overestimate peak amplitudes and underestimate external noise, the results were highly comparable to measurements and consistent with the physical expectations. These findings provide a robust foundation for enhancing data-driven condition monitoring methods, particularly those employing machine learning or deep learning.

中文摘要翻译: 齿轮是机电应用中不可或缺的组成部分，但准确的状态监测方法（包括数据驱动的预测性维护）强烈依赖于高质量的数据，尤其是来自故障部件的数据。为解决数据稀缺问题，我们提出了一种采用先进多边形接触方法的多体仿真，用于复现来自实验性方位推力器测试台的扭转振动。关键的创新点在于能够模拟具有任意故障几何形状的健康齿轮和故障齿轮。仿真信号在时域和频域上均与测量结果高度吻合。在时域中，平均扭矩水平和周期性波动匹配良好，尽管测量信号表现出更高的峰峰值幅值和更大的噪声，尤其是在较低转速的健康工况下。在频域中，仿真准确地再现了预期的故障频率及其对应的边带，且较大故障产生更高的幅值。虽然仿真倾向于高估峰值幅值并低估外部噪声，但结果与测量值高度可比，并且符合物理预期。这些发现为增强数据驱动的状态监测方法（特别是采用机器学习或深度学习方法）提供了坚实基础。

</details>


### [206] [Thermal characterisation by Scanning Photothermal Radiometry using a random undersampled measurement scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13046)
*Florian Crouau,Alejandro Mateos-Canseco,Jérémie Maire,Jean-Luc Battaglia,Stéphane Chevalier*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出在扫描光热辐射测量（SPR）中对稀疏信号采用非均匀采样与加权随机技术，成功将碳纤维-铝基复合材料的测量量减少6倍，同时保持检测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SPR扫描方法耗时较长，需要减少测量次数以提高效率。

Method: 对稀疏信号采用非均匀采样（irregular sampling），并结合加权随机技术（weighted random technique）进一步减少所需样本量。

Result: 在使用SPR对碳纤维-铝基复合材料样品进行检测时，可将测量次数减少6倍。

Conclusion: 通过非均匀采样和加权随机方法，可以在不显著牺牲检测质量的前提下大幅减少SPR测量时间，提高检测效率。

Original Abstract: Scanning Photothermal Radiometry (SPR) is an active thermal technique that is simultaneously non-destructive, contactless, and allows for temporal resolutions on the order of nanoseconds, spatial resolutions down to the sub-micrometre scale and at different depths. This scanning method can be time consuming thus this work shows that it is possible to reduce the amount of measurements taken by 6 when using SPR on a sample consisting of carbon fibres in an aluminium matrix. It uses irregular sampling on sparse signals, and a weighted random technique to further decrease the amount of samples needed.

中文摘要翻译: 扫描光热辐射测量（SPR）是一种主动热学技术，具有非破坏性、非接触性，同时可实现纳秒级时间分辨率、亚微米级空间分辨率以及不同深度的检测能力。由于该扫描方法较为耗时，本文证明了在对碳纤维-铝基复合材料样品进行SPR检测时，可以将测量次数减少6倍。该方法对稀疏信号采用非均匀采样，并利用加权随机技术进一步降低所需样本量。

</details>


### [207] [Inverse Learning assisted V2I Communication for Intent Based 6G ISAC Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13091)
*Anoop C,Anup Aprem*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对6G车联网中RSU融入自主意图驱动范式和通感一体化能力带来的新挑战，提出了基于逆学习的框架来推断RSU的效用函数（即意图），并设计了非参数与参数化方法，以及联邦学习版本，用于V2I通信应用（如协作数据下载和动态簇头选择）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 6G将引入意图驱动网络范式和通感一体化能力，使RSU成为自主认知智能体。这种范式转变要求V2I通信策略能够学习和适应RSU的感知辅助通信行为及其自主决策策略，因此需要推断RSU背后的效用函数（意图）。

Method: (i) ATIL：基于Afriat定理的非参数化固定效用学习方法；(ii) FICNNIL：基于全输入凹神经网络的参数化固定效用学习方法；(iii) PICNNIL：基于部分输入凹神经网络的参数化状态依赖效用逆学习方法；(iv) FedFICNNIL和FedPICNNIL：分别用于固定效用和状态依赖效用的联邦逆学习算法。

Result: 在车载微云场景下的两个V2I通信应用（预测性协作数据下载调度和动态簇头选择）中验证了所提出的逆学习框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于逆学习的框架能够有效推断RSU的意图（效用函数），为6G车联网中V2I通信策略的设计提供了新的范式，使通信策略能够自适应RSU的自主决策和感知辅助通信行为。

Original Abstract: 6G is expected to bring unprecedented advancements in the capabilities of vehicular networks. However, the advent of 6G will also introduce changes in the operation of vehicular communication infrastructures such as roadside units (RSUs), including the incorporation of autonomous intent-based network paradigm and integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) capabilities. While ISAC enables sensing and communication within a single 6G network node, intent-based network design paradigm ensures that network nodes such as RSUs, act as autonomous cognitive agents to fulfill the objectives of their respective communication service providers. This paradigm shift necessitates the development of V2I communication strategies that learns and adapts to the sensing-assisted communication and the autonomous decision-making strategies of RSUs. We model the RSU as a constrained utility maximizer, where the utility function characterizes the RSU intent, and formulate an inverse learning (IL) problem to infer the underlying utility function from observed ISAC RSU actions, for example the adaptive beamwidth allocation in response to the kinematic states of vehicles within a vehicular micro-cloud (VMC). The main contributions of this paper are: (i) ATIL, a nonparametric method based on Afriat theorem for fixed utility learning; (ii) FICNNIL, a parametric approach using fully input-concave neural networks, for structured fixed utility learning; and (iii) PICNNIL, a parametric approach based on partially input-concave neural networks, for inverse learning of state-dependent utilities. (iv) Federated inverse learning algorithms FedFICNNIL and FedPICNNIL for fixed and state dependent utility, respectively. We demonstrate the proposed IL-based framework for two V2I communication applications in VMCs, namely predictive scheduling for cooperative data downloading and dynamic cluster-head selection.

中文摘要翻译: 6G有望为车联网带来前所未有的能力提升。然而，6G的出现也将改变路边单元（RSU）等车载通信基础设施的运作方式，包括融入自主意图驱动网络范式和通感一体化（ISAC）能力。通感一体化使单个6G网络节点能够同时进行感知和通信，而意图驱动网络设计范式则确保RSU等网络节点作为自主认知智能体，以实现各自通信服务提供商的目标。这种范式转变要求开发能够学习和适应感知辅助通信以及RSU自主决策策略的车对基础设施（V2I）通信策略。我们将RSU建模为受约束的效用最大化器，其中效用函数刻画了RSU的意图，并构建了一个逆学习（IL）问题，从观测到的ISAC RSU动作中推断潜在的效用函数，例如车辆微云（VMC）中车辆运动状态驱动的自适应波束宽度分配。本文的主要贡献包括：(i) ATIL，一种基于Afriat定理的非参数化固定效用学习方法；(ii) FICNNIL，一种使用全输入凹神经网络的参数化固定效用学习方法；(iii) PICNNIL，一种基于部分输入凹神经网络的参数化状态依赖效用逆学习方法；(iv) 针对固定效用和状态依赖效用的联邦逆学习算法FedFICNNIL和FedPICNNIL。我们在VMC中的两个V2I通信应用（即预测性协作数据下载调度和动态簇头选择）上展示了所提出的逆学习框架的有效性。

</details>


### [208] [A High Input Impedance Chopper Stabilized Amplifier Based On Charge Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13129)
*Prabhas K Deshpande,Naveen Kadayinti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种差分电容翻转技术，用于斩波型电容耦合仪表放大器（CCIA），以提升其输入阻抗。该技术通过在每个周期中重新配置电容位置，避免输入电容的充放电，从而将输入阻抗从与斩波频率相关的阻性变为纯容性（与频率无关），实现了高达21 GOhm的DC输入阻抗。该电路在TSMC 65nm工艺下实现，功耗2.6μW，输入参考噪声7.2μVrms（1-150 Hz），适用于干电极ECG信号采集等传感器接口场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统斩波稳定放大器由于开关电容网络导致输入阻抗较低，且与斩波频率成反比。当与高输出阻抗传感器（如干电极）接口时，会加载传感器并降低灵敏度。因此需要一种能够在不牺牲斩波操作的前提下提升输入阻抗的技术。

Method: 提出差分电容翻转（Differential Capacitor Flipping）技术，在斩波运算的每个周期中重新配置输入电容的位置，防止电容在每个周期中被完全放电和重新充电，从而将输入阻抗从阻性（与Fch和Ci乘积成反比）转变为纯容性（与Fch无关）。

Result: 在TSMC 65 nm CMOS工艺下实现：DC输入阻抗为21 GOhm；功耗2.6μW（含时钟生成电路为2.8μW）；总集成输入参考噪声7.2μVrms（1 Hz-150 Hz）；成功用于干电极（输出阻抗数MOhm）的ECG信号采集。

Conclusion: 提出的差分电容翻转技术有效解决了斩波稳定放大器输入阻抗低的问题，在保持斩波噪声抑制优势的同时实现了极高的输入阻抗，特别适合与高输出阻抗传感器（如干电极）接口的生物电信号采集应用。

Original Abstract: Chopper stabilized amplifiers are popularly used for realizing amplifiers with low offset and for rejecting flicker noise. One of the main limitations of these amplifiers is the low Input Impedance (Zin) produced by the switch capacitor input network. Zin here is resistive due to the switch capacitor action and is inversely proportional to the product of Chopping frequency (Fch) and Input Capacitance (Ci). Since Fch should be greater than the flicker noise corner frequency, this results in a low Zin. When interfacing sensors with high Sensor Output Impedance (Zo), chopper stabilized amplifiers load the sensors resulting in reduced sensitivity. This paper presents a novel input impedance boosting technique - Differential capacitor flipping technique for chopper based Capacitively Coupled Instrumentation Amplifier (CCIA), which prevents discharge and recharge of Ci's in every cycle by reconfiguring the capacitor positions while preserving the chopping operation. This ideally results in a purely capacitive Zin which is independent of Fch. The proposed architecture is used to demonstrate Electrocardiogram (ECG) signal acquisition with dry electrodes that have Zo in the order of a few Mega Ohms. This circuit implemented in TSMC 65 nm CMOS technology node features Zin of 21 GOhms at DC. The circuit has a power consumption of 2.6E(-6)W (2.8E(-6)W including clock generation circuits), with 7.2E(-6)Vrms (1 Hz-150 Hz) of total integrated input referred noise. ~

中文摘要翻译: 斩波稳定放大器广泛用于实现低失调和抑制闪烁噪声的放大器。这类放大器的主要限制之一是开关电容输入网络产生的低输入阻抗（Zin）。由于开关电容作用，这里的Zin呈阻性，且与斩波频率（Fch）和输入电容（Ci）的乘积成反比。由于Fch必须大于闪烁噪声转角频率，这导致Zin较低。当与具有高传感器输出阻抗（Zo）的传感器接口时，斩波稳定放大器会加载传感器，导致灵敏度降低。本文提出一种新颖的输入阻抗提升技术——基于斩波的电容耦合仪表放大器（CCIA）的差分电容翻转技术，该技术通过在每个周期中重新配置电容位置，在保持斩波操作的同时防止Ci的放电和再充电。这理想地产生了与Fch无关的纯容性Zin。所提出的架构用于演示使用干电极（其Zo在数兆欧量级）的心电图（ECG）信号采集。该电路采用TSMC 65 nm CMOS工艺技术节点实现，具有21 GOhm的DC输入阻抗。电路功耗为2.6E(-6)W（含时钟生成电路为2.8E(-6)W），总集成输入参考噪声为7.2E(-6)Vrms（1 Hz-150 Hz）。

</details>


### [209] [The Influence of Gain and Phase Mismatches on Beam Patterns in Phased Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13378)
*Jérémy Guichemerre,Christoph Studer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对相控阵天线因增益、相位和延迟失配导致的旁瓣电平恶化问题，提出了一种频域分析框架，揭示了失配误差对波束图的影响机制，并推导了最大旁瓣电平的近似分布，实现了面向良率的设计和快速设计空间探索。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究要么分析特定结构的失配模式，要么推导随机失配下的每角度边际统计量，但无法刻画全局波束图指标（如最大旁瓣电平），因此需要一种能够全局表征失配影响的框架。

Method: 提出频域分析框架，将波束图描述为锥削窗依赖的基函数沿阵列结构和信号带宽决定的变形方向上的评估。通过谱分析揭示单元误差产生理想波束图的加权副本，其幅度由失配序列的离散傅里叶变换给出，并基于此推导随机增益和相位失配下最大旁瓣电平的近似分布。

Result: 推导出的表达式能够实现面向良率的设计和快速设计空间探索，无需依赖计算密集的蒙特卡洛仿真。

Conclusion: 该频域框架有效解决了相控阵失配下全局波束图指标难以分析的问题，为实际系统设计提供了高效的分析工具。

Original Abstract: Practical implementations of phased arrays suffer from per-antenna gain, phase, and delay mismatches, which can significantly worsen the maximum sidelobe level (SLL) of beampatterns. The existing literature either analyzes specific structured mismatch patterns or derives per-angle marginal statistics under random mismatches, which fail to characterize global beampattern metrics such as the maximum SLL. To address this limitation, we propose a frequency-domain framework in which the beampattern is described by a tapering-window-dependent base function evaluated along a deformation determined by the array architecture and signal bandwidth. This formulation enables a spectral analysis of mismatches, revealing that element-wise errors generate weighted replicas of the ideal beampattern whose amplitudes are given by the discrete Fourier transform of the mismatch sequence. Building on this insight, we derive an approximation of the maximum SLL distribution under random gain and phase mismatches. The resulting expressions enable yield-oriented design and rapid design-space exploration without relying on computationally intensive Monte-Carlo simulations.

中文摘要翻译: 相控阵的实际实现中，每根天线的增益、相位和延迟失配会严重恶化波束图的最大旁瓣电平（SLL）。现有文献要么分析特定的结构化失配模式，要么推导随机失配下每角度的边际统计量，但均无法刻画最大SLL等全局波束图指标。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一种频域框架，在该框架下，波束图被描述为一个依赖于锥削窗的基函数，沿由阵列架构和信号带宽决定的变形方向上进行评估。该公式实现了对失配的谱分析，揭示了单元误差生成理想波束图的加权副本，其幅度由失配序列的离散傅里叶变换给出。基于这一洞察，我们推导了随机增益和相位失配下最大SLL分布的近似表达式。所得表达式能够实现面向良率的设计和快速设计空间探索，而无需依赖计算密集的蒙特卡洛仿真。

</details>


### [210] [Towards Standardizing Affine Frequency Division Multiplexing (AFDM) for Future Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13416)
*Qu Luo,Lixia Xiao,Pei Xiao,Zilong Liu,Yin Xu,Qihao Peng,Zeping Sui,Hee Wook Kim,Hüseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文从标准化视角系统研究了仿射频分复用（AFDM）波形，分析其与4G/5G多参数集、FMCW雷达波形和LoRa调制的后向兼容性，讨论多天线、多用户支持和PAPR等关键标准化能力，并探索其在NTN、ISAC、V2X和水声通信等新兴场景中的潜力，表明AFDM是未来无线网络的一项及时且有前景的技术。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AFDM波形在抗双选信道和通感一体化方面具有优势，但缺乏从标准化视角对其进行的系统性研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，为AFDM在未来无线网络中的标准化应用提供系统分析。

Method: 采用系统性综述与分析方法：首先介绍AFDM原理及波形标准化考虑；其次分析AFDM与4G/5G多参数集、FMCW雷达波形、LoRa调制的后向兼容性；然后讨论多天线、多用户支持和PAPR等标准化关键能力；最后探索AFDM在NTN、ISAC、V2X和水声通信等新兴场景中的潜力。

Result: AFDM可以以有限修改集成到现有处理链中；在多天线、多用户支持和PAPR等标准化关键方面具有竞争力；在NTN、ISAC、V2X和水声通信等面临严重时延-多普勒色散的新兴场景中表现出良好的波形鲁棒性潜力。

Conclusion: AFDM是一种及时且具前景的未来无线网络波形技术，具备良好的标准化基础和广泛的应用潜力。

Original Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing~(AFDM) has emerged as a compelling waveform candidate for future wireless networks, owing to its strong resilience to doubly selective channels and its ability to enable the seamless integration of communication and sensing functionalities. Against this context, this article provides a systematic study of AFDM from a standardization perspective. We first introduce the principles of AFDM and discuss the major considerations involved in waveform standardization. We then examine the backwards compatibility of AFDM with 4G/5G multi-numerology frameworks and their anticipated evolution, frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar waveforms, and long-range (LoRa) modulation, demonstrating that AFDM can be incorporated into legacy processing chains with limited modification. Key standardization-critical capabilities are further discussed, including multiple-antenna and multi-user support, and peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR). Finally, we investigate the potential of AFDM in several emerging scenarios, including non-terrestrial networks~(NTN), integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), vehicle-to-everything (V2X), and underwater acoustic (UWA) communications, whereby severe delay-Doppler dispersion places stringent demands on waveform robustness. Through these explorations, it is shown that that AFDM represents a timely and compelling technology for future wireless networks.

中文摘要翻译: 仿射频分复用（AFDM）凭借其对双选信道的强大鲁棒性以及实现通信与感知功能无缝融合的能力，已成为未来无线网络中有竞争力的波形候选方案。在此背景下，本文从标准化视角对AFDM进行了系统性研究。我们首先介绍了AFDM的原理，并讨论了波形标准化中的主要考量因素。然后，我们考察了AFDM与4G/5G多参数集及其预期演进、调频连续波（FMCW）雷达波形以及远距离（LoRa）调制的后向兼容性，结果表明AFDM可以以有限的修改集成到现有处理链中。进一步讨论了多项标准化关键能力，包括多天线和多用户支持以及峰值平均功率比（PAPR）。最后，我们研究了AFDM在非地面网络（NTN）、通感一体化（ISAC）、车联万物（V2X）以及水声（UWA）通信等几个新兴场景中的潜力，这些场景中严重的时延-多普勒色散对波形的鲁棒性提出了严苛要求。通过这些探索，本文表明AFDM是未来无线网络中一项及时且具有吸引力的技术。

</details>


### [211] [Mitigating SAR-ADC Non-Idealities in Massive MU-MIMO Systems via Affine Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13417)
*Jérémy Guichemerre,Christoph Studer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模MU-MIMO系统中低分辨率数据转换器的非理想特性，提出了两种仿射模型（基于Bussgang分解和最大化SDR）来模拟SAR ADC的失配、电容非线性等非理想因素，并设计了低复杂度的方法来缓解这些非理想性对系统性能的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文献几乎都专注于理想化的量化模型，忽略了实际ADC实现中存在的固有非理想特性，这限制了低分辨率数据转换器在大规模MU-MIMO系统中的实际应用效果。

Method: 提出了两种仿射模型：一种基于Bussgang分解，另一种通过最大化信号失真比（SDR）来建模；利用这些模型设计了低复杂度的算法来减轻SAR ADC非理想性对大规模MU-MIMO无线系统的影响。

Result: 所提出的两种仿射模型能够有效描述SAR ADC的主要非理想特性，并且基于这些模型设计的低复杂度方法能够显著缓解非理想性带来的性能损失。

Conclusion: 通过使用所提出的仿射模型和相应的低复杂度缓解方法，可以在实际大规模MU-MIMO系统中有效利用低分辨率ADC的优势，同时克服其非理想特性带来的挑战。

Original Abstract: Low-resolution data converters can significantly reduce the power consumption and silicon area of all-digital massive multi-user (MU) multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) basestations. However, the existing literature almost exclusively focuses on idealistic quantization models, neglecting the inherent non-idealities present in real-world analog-to-digital converter (ADC) implementations. To overcome this limitation, we propose two affine models, one based on Bussgang's decomposition and one that maximizes the signal-to-distortion ratio (SDR), both accounting for the most prominent non-idealities in successive approximation register (SAR) ADCs. Subsequently, we utilize these models to devise low-complexity methods that mitigate SAR-ADC non-idealities in massive MU-MIMO wireless systems.

中文摘要翻译: 低分辨率数据转换器可以显著降低全数字大规模多用户多输入多输出（MU-MIMO）基站的功耗和硅面积。然而，现有文献几乎完全专注于理想化的量化模型，忽略了实际模数转换器（ADC）实现中存在的固有非理想特性。为克服这一局限性，我们提出了两种仿射模型——一种基于Bussgang分解，另一种通过最大化信号失真比（SDR）来建模——两者均考虑了逐次逼近寄存器（SAR）ADC中最显著的非理想特性。随后，我们利用这些模型设计了低复杂度的方法，以减轻大规模MU-MIMO无线系统中SAR ADC非理想性的影响。

</details>


### [212] [Max-Min Secrecy Rate Optimization for Secure ISAC Networks: Global Optimization and Low-Complexity Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13582)
*Thanh-Nha To,Trung Quang Pham,Dang Y Hoang,Hoang-Lai Pham,Tuan Anh Pham*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究存在不可信感知用户（可能窃听通信用户机密信息）的保密集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，提出基于分支定界（BB）的全局最优算法和基于逐次凸逼近（SCA）的低复杂度算法，在功率预算和感知质量约束下最大化最小保密率，实现安全公平性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在ISAC系统中，感知用户（SUs）可能窃听通信用户（CUs）的机密信息，现有研究缺乏针对此类安全威胁的公平性保障机制，因此需要设计兼顾安全公平性、感知质量和计算效率的优化方案。

Method: 首先利用半定松弛（SDR）重构原问题；然后提出分支定界（BB）框架结合凸松弛获得全局最优解；为降低复杂度，进一步提出基于逐次凸逼近（SCA）的低复杂度算法，迭代求解一系列凸子问题并收敛到局部解。

Result: BB算法能获得全局最优解，可作为性能评估基准；SCA算法以显著更低的计算复杂度达到接近最优的保密性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的BB算法为ISAC安全性能评估提供了基准，而SCA算法在计算复杂度和保密性能之间取得了良好折中，适用于实际ISAC部署。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a secure integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system in which multiple communication users (CUs) coexist with multiple untrusted sensing users (SUs) that may eavesdrop on the confidential information intended for the CUs. To promote security fairness among users, we formulate a max-min secrecy rate optimization problem subject to a transmit power budget and sensing quality requirements characterized by beampattern matching error constraints. The resulting design problem is highly non-convex due to the secrecy rate expressions and non-convex sensing constraints. To address these challenges, we first reformulate the problem using semidefinite relaxation (SDR). Based on the reformulated problem, we develop a branch-and-bound (BB) framework combined with convex relaxations to obtain the globally optimal solution within a prescribed accuracy. To further reduce computational complexity, we propose a low-complexity algorithm based on successive convex approximation (SCA), which iteratively solves a sequence of convex subproblems and converges to a local solution. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed BB algorithm achieves the global optimum and provides a benchmark for performance evaluation. Moreover, the proposed SCA-based algorithm attains near-optimal secrecy performance with significantly lower computational complexity, making it attractive for practical ISAC deployments.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一个安全的集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，其中多个通信用户（CUs）与多个不可信的感知用户（SUs）共存，这些感知用户可能窃听旨在传输给通信用户的机密信息。为了提升用户间的安全公平性，我们构建了一个最大-最小保密率优化问题，该问题受限于发射功率预算和由波束图匹配误差约束表征的感知质量要求。由于保密率表达式和非凸感知约束的存在，所得到的设计问题高度非凸。为应对这些挑战，我们首先利用半定松弛（SDR）对问题进行重构。基于重构后的问题，我们开发了一个结合凸松弛的分支定界（BB）框架，以在指定精度内获得全局最优解。为进一步降低计算复杂度，我们提出了一种基于逐次凸逼近（SCA）的低复杂度算法，该算法迭代求解一系列凸子问题并收敛到一个局部解。数值结果表明，所提出的BB算法能够获得全局最优解，并为性能评估提供了基准。此外，基于SCA的算法能以显著更低的计算复杂度实现接近最优的保密性能，使其适用于实际的ISAC部署场景。

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [A quantum-like benchmark for context-sensitive associative memory with adaptive plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12449)
*Yashine H. Goolam Hossen,Lea Gassab,Travis J. A. Craddock*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于阶段关联回忆的顺序敏感自适应可塑性基准，通过比较类量子关联记忆模型与经典对照模型，发现多目标性能指标（回忆、时间组织、上下文敏感性）比单一回忆得分更能区分模型类别，且自适应可塑性（尤其是稳态稳定化）是获得有用回忆增益的关键。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 学习和记忆需要在可塑性与稳定性之间取得平衡。已有联想记忆模型在固定背景连接已承担部分任务时可能表现虚假成功，难以区分真正回忆动态与结构性辅助。本文旨在通过受控基准测试，在弱结构支持、可塑性调节和匹配经典对照的条件下，严格评估不同模型家族的回忆能力。

Method: 设计了一个顺序敏感的自适应可塑性基准，用于阶段关联回忆任务。比较了类量子关联记忆模型与匹配的实值无相位模型和马尔可夫速率对照模型，在相同任务调度、扰动轮廓、弱支持条件和可塑性设置下进行对比分析。先筛选弱结构支持，再固定保守工作点进行因子比较。

Result: 弱支持的可用范围狭窄且非单调；无可塑性消融实验中弱结构无法单独挽救回忆；大多数有用回忆增益来自自适应可塑性，尤其是稳态稳定化；马尔可夫速率对照模型常获得更强的原始回忆，但类量子模型更一致地保持顺序敏感性和阶段依赖性组织；类量子模型并无普遍优势。

Conclusion: 模型类别应通过多目标性能指标（回忆、时间组织、上下文敏感性）来区分，而非单一回忆得分。该基准为在弱支持、可塑性调节和匹配经典比较条件下研究上下文敏感的记忆动力学提供了受控框架。

Original Abstract: Learning and memory require a balance between plasticity and stability: synaptic connections must encode new information without collapsing, saturating, or erasing previously useful structure. Associative-memory models can appear to learn successfully when fixed background connectivity already carries part of the task, making it difficult to distinguish genuine recall dynamics from structural assistance. We test this issue using an order-sensitive adaptive-plasticity benchmark for staged associative recall. The benchmark compares a quantum-like associative-memory model with matched real-valued no-phase and Markov-rate controls under the same task schedule, perturbation profiles, weak-support conditions, and plasticity settings. Here, "quantum-like" refers to the modeling formalism, not to a biological claim about quantum computation. We first screen weak structural support and then fix a conservative operating point for factorial comparisons across model families and plasticity mechanisms. The useful weak-support regime is narrow and non-monotonic. Weak structure alone does not rescue recall in the no-plasticity ablation, whereas most useful recall gains arise from adaptive plasticity, especially homeostatic stabilization. The Markov-rate control often achieves stronger raw recall, but the quantum-like model more consistently preserves order sensitivity and stage-dependent organization. These results do not support a universal quantum-like advantage. Instead, they show that model classes are better distinguished by a multi-objective profile combining recall, temporal organization, and context sensitivity than by any single recall score. The benchmark therefore provides a controlled framework for studying context-sensitive memory dynamics under weak support, regulated plasticity, and matched classical comparison.

中文摘要翻译: 学习和记忆需要在可塑性与稳定性之间取得平衡：突触连接必须编码新信息，而不会崩溃、饱和或擦除先前有用的结构。当固定背景连接已承担部分任务时，联想记忆模型可能看似成功学习，这使得真正回忆动态与结构性辅助难以区分。我们采用一个针对阶段关联回忆的顺序敏感自适应可塑性基准来测试这一问题。该基准将类量子联想记忆模型与匹配的实值无相位模型和马尔可夫速率对照模型在相同任务调度、扰动轮廓、弱支持条件和可塑性设置下进行比较。此处，“类量子”指建模形式，而非关于量子计算的生物学主张。我们首先筛选弱结构支持，然后固定一个保守工作点，以进行跨模型家族和可塑性机制的因子比较。弱支持的有用范围狭窄且非单调。在无可塑性消融实验中，弱结构单独无法挽救回忆，而大多数有用的回忆增益来自自适应可塑性，尤其是稳态稳定化。马尔可夫速率对照模型常获得更强的原始回忆，但类量子模型更一致地保持顺序敏感性和阶段依赖性组织。这些结果不支持普遍的类量子优势。相反，它们表明模型类别更宜通过结合回忆、时间组织和上下文敏感性的多目标指标来区分，而非通过任何单一回忆得分。因此，该基准为在弱支持、可塑性调节和匹配经典比较条件下研究上下文敏感的记忆动力学提供了受控框架。

</details>


### [214] [Multifractal human signals at the edge of life reveal a heart-brain anti-correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12600)
*Yago Emanoel Ramos,Maria Eloá do Ó,Henrique Ferraz de Arruda,Mauro Copelli,G. Camelo-Neto,Pedro V. Carelli*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过多分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）发现，临终阶段人类神经和心脏系统的多分形谱呈现相反的演变趋势：神经活动多分形性坍缩，心脏信号异常谱宽增加，表明功能性解耦和体-脑崩溃。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究临终过程中人体生理功能的非线性动力学崩溃机制，特别是神经与心脏系统之间协调关系的解体过程。

Method: 采用多分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）方法，量化临终患者同步采集的脑电图（EEG）和心电图（ECG）时间序列中复杂性的时间演化特征。

Result: 神经活动呈现多分形性坍缩，趋向更受限状态；心脏信号出现异常谱宽增加，表明非线性波动增强和动力学不稳定性增加；两种谱宽呈负相关，表明功能性解耦和反相关动态的出现。

Conclusion: 临终过程并非统一的生理衰退，而是体-脑崩溃（body-to-brain breakdown）过程，即外周功能障碍逐渐压垮中枢调控机制，表现为跨系统整合的极端解体形式，层级协调机制崩溃。

Original Abstract: This study investigates the terminal breakdown of human neurophysiological function through the lens of non-linear dynamics by analyzing the multifractal spectrum. Using Multifractal Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (MF-DFA), we quantify the temporal evolution of complexity in synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG) and electrocardiogram (ECG) time series from patients in the terminal stage. Our results reveal a marked divergence in multifractal spectrum width: while neural activity exhibits a collapse of multifractality toward a more constrained state, cardiac signals undergo anomalous spectral broadening, indicating increased non-linear fluctuations and dynamical instability. A negative correlation between these spectral widths suggests effective functional decoupling and the emergence of anti-correlated dynamics between neural and cardiac systems. Rather than reflecting a uniform physiological decline, this divergence is consistent with a body-to-brain breakdown in which peripheral dysfunction progressively overwhelms central regulatory processes. In a broader context, the observed opposing trends resemble patterns reported in other body-driven adaptive processes, suggesting that inverse dynamics across coupled systems may emerge when constraints originate from peripheral rather than central mechanisms. Ultimately, the dying process appears to represent an extreme form of cross-system disintegration, marked by the collapse of the hierarchical coordination that normally sustains integrated physiological function.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究通过非线性动力学的视角，利用多分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）方法，探究人类临终阶段神经生理功能的终极崩溃。我们分析了临终患者同步采集的脑电图（EEG）与心电图（ECG）时间序列中复杂性的时间演化特征。结果揭示了一个显著的多分形谱宽度的分歧现象：神经活动呈现出多分形性坍缩，趋向于更受限的状态；而心脏信号则出现异常谱宽增加，表明非线性波动增强和动力学不稳定性上升。这两种谱宽度之间存在负相关关系，提示神经与心脏系统之间发生了有效的功能性解耦，并出现了反相关动力学。这一分歧并非反映统一的生理衰退，而是与一种"体-脑崩溃"（body-to-brain breakdown）过程相一致，其中外周功能障碍逐渐压垮中枢调控机制。在更广泛的背景下，观察到的相反趋势与其他外周驱动适应过程中报道的模式类似，表明当约束源于外周而非中枢机制时，耦合系统之间可能出现反向动力学。最终，临终过程似乎代表了一种极端形式的跨系统解体，其标志是维持综合生理功能所需的层级协调机制的崩溃。

</details>


### [215] [Phase model analysis of the effect of M-current on neural synchrony in hippocampal networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12684)
*Megha Manoj,Sue Ann Campbell*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 通过相位模型约简，研究乙酰胆碱（ACh）通过调节M电流对海马体中神经集群同步与去同步的双向调控作用，揭示低ACh促进全同步（记忆巩固），高ACh导致多稳定对称集群（记忆编码）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 验证关于乙酰胆碱水平高低分别促进记忆编码与巩固的假说，从神经同步动力学层面解释ACh如何调控海马体中神经集群的形成与转换。

Method: 构建包含M电流的锥体神经元网络模型，利用弱耦合一对神经元的一维相位模型约简，预测全局均匀、对称距离依赖和最近邻三种耦合结构下可能出现的对称集群解。

Result: 低ACh（M电流强）条件下网络可达到完全同步；高ACh（M电流弱）条件下网络去同步，形成多个稳定的对称集群解，代表不同的神经集群。

Conclusion: ACh通过调节M电流双向控制海马体神经网络的同步程度：低水平促进全同步支持记忆巩固，高水平促使网络分裂为多个集群支持记忆编码。

Original Abstract: Neural assemblies, transiently coordinated groups of neurons, observed in the hippocampus are thought to underlie the formation of episodic memories. Acetylcholine (ACh), a neuromodulator, that is received by the hippocampus, plays a critical role in memory and learning. A well supported hypothesis suggests that high levels of ACh during active exploration and rapid eye movement (REM) sleep promote memory encoding, while low levels during quiet waking and slow-wave sleep (SWS) support memory consolidation. We study this bidirectional role of ACh in neural assembly formation through its effect on the synchrony among neurons. We consider a network model of pyramidal neurons, each equipped with a slow, voltage-dependent, non-inactivating potassium current (M-current), which is downregulated in the presence of ACh. Neural assemblies are represented as cluster solutions to this system. Using a one-dimensional phase model reduction of a pair of weakly coupled pyramidal neurons under different levels of the M-current, we predict the symmetric cluster solutions that may emerge in larger networks equipped with all-to-all globally homogeneous, symmetric distance-dependent and nearest-neighbours coupling architectures. We find that under low ACh conditions, the network can fully synchronize, whereas high levels can desynchronize the network into multiple stable symmetric cluster solutions representing distinct neural assemblies.

中文摘要翻译: 在海马体中观察到的神经集群（即短暂协调的神经元群体）被认为是形成情景记忆的基础。海马体接收的神经调质乙酰胆碱（ACh）在记忆和学习中发挥着关键作用。一个得到广泛支持的假说认为，在主动探索和快速眼动（REM）睡眠期间的高水平ACh促进记忆编码，而在安静清醒和慢波睡眠（SWS）期间的低水平ACh支持记忆巩固。我们通过ACh对神经元同步性的影响，研究其在神经集群形成中的双向作用。我们构建了一个锥体神经元网络模型，每个神经元都配备一种缓慢的、电压依赖的、非失活的钾电流（M电流），该电流在ACh存在时被下调。神经集群被表示为该系统的集群解。利用一对弱耦合锥体神经元在不同M电流水平下的一维相位模型约简，我们预测了在配备全连接全局均匀、对称距离依赖和最近邻耦合结构的大型网络中可能出现的对称集群解。我们发现，在低ACh条件下，网络可以实现完全同步，而高水平ACh则会使网络去同步，形成多个稳定的对称集群解，代表不同的神经集群。

</details>


### [216] [Deep Sleep Classification via EEG Signal Criticality: A Passive BCI Approach for Sleep-Improvement Neurofeedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13017)
*Stanisław Narębski,Tomasz Komendziński,Tomasz M. Rutkowski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于去趋势波动分析（DFA）的临界性特征用于深度睡眠（N3）识别，通过UMAP流形学习和六种分类器基准测试发现，朴素贝叶斯在平衡准确率上表现最佳（87.17%），显著优于深度神经网络和随机森林，证明了DFA临界性特征可作为pBCI高精度状态感知机制，支持目标听觉刺激等神经反馈应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自动睡眠分期是被动脑机接口（pBCI）的基础应用，旨在解码自发神经状态以实现不依赖用户意图的闭环干预。本研究聚焦于利用DFA提取的临界性特征特异性识别深度睡眠（N3期），以开发高精度神经反馈状态感知引擎。

Method: 分析了290名老年女性的347,232个EEG时段，使用UMAP流形学习可视化状态转换；随后通过10折交叉验证对六种分类器进行基准测试，以平衡准确率评估最优的"状态感知"引擎。

Result: 朴素贝叶斯取得了最高的平均平衡准确率（87.17% ± 0.24%），显著优于全连接深度神经网络（FNN: 81.58%）和随机森林（80.97%）；线性模型（LDA: 57.21%；SVM: 51.01%）表现较差，表明DFA临界性特征位于独特的非线性流形上。

Conclusion: EEG临界性的概率解码为pBCI提供了高精度感知机制。该鲁棒分类流水线支持开发状态依赖的神经反馈，如靶向听觉刺激，以促进认知恢复。

Original Abstract: Automated sleep staging is a fundamental application of passive Brain-Computer Interfaces (pBCI), decoding spontaneous neural states to enable closed-loop interventions independent of user intent. This study evaluates criticality features derived from Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (DFA) for the specific identification of deep sleep (N3).
  We analyzed $347,232$ EEG epochs from $290$ older women using UMAP manifold learning to visualize state transitions. Subsequently, six classifiers were benchmarked via 10-fold cross-validation, using balanced accuracy to determine the optimal "state-sensing" engine for neurofeedback.Naive Bayes achieved the highest mean balanced accuracy ($87.17\% \pm 0.24\%$), significantly outperforming a fully connected deep neural network (FNN: $81.58\%$) and Random Forest ($80.97\%$). Linear models (LDA: $57.21\%$; SVM: $51.01\%$) performed poorly, indicating that DFA-derived criticality features reside on a distinct, non-linear manifold.
  Probabilistic decoding of EEG criticality provides a high-accuracy sensing mechanism for pBCIs. This robust classification pipeline supports the development of state-dependent neurofeedback, such as targeted auditory stimulation, to enhance cognitive recovery.

中文摘要翻译: 自动睡眠分期是被动脑机接口（pBCI）的一项基础应用，通过对自发神经状态进行解码，实现不依赖用户意图的闭环干预。本研究评估了基于去趋势波动分析（DFA）提取的临界性特征在特异性识别深度睡眠（N3期）中的性能。我们分析了290名老年女性的347,232个EEG时段，利用UMAP流形学习对状态转换进行可视化。随后，通过10折交叉验证对六种分类器进行基准测试，以平衡准确率为指标确定神经反馈的最优"状态感知"引擎。朴素贝叶斯取得了最高的平均平衡准确率（87.17% ± 0.24%），显著优于全连接深度神经网络（FNN: 81.58%）和随机森林（80.97%）。线性模型（LDA: 57.21%；SVM: 51.01%）表现较差，表明DFA衍生的临界性特征位于一个独特的、非线性的流形上。EEG临界性的概率解码为pBCI提供了一种高精度的感知机制。这一鲁棒分类流水线支持开发状态依赖的神经反馈，例如靶向听觉刺激，以促进认知恢复。

</details>


### [217] [Including the Cost of Irreducible Uncertainty in the Policy Compression Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13132)
*Álvaro Garrido-Pérez,Pieter Simoens,Amrapali Pednekar,Yara Khaluf*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文指出政策压缩框架忽略了条件熵（给定状态下选择动作的不可约不确定性）的认知成本，通过引入加权条件熵项扩展该框架，使最优策略更尖锐，能更灵活地解释人类决策偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有政策压缩框架将认知成本定义为状态与动作之间的互信息（策略复杂度），但忽略了对条件熵（给定状态下应选择哪个动作的不可约不确定性）的度量，而经验证据表明条件熵会影响反应时。作者因此认为该框架不完整，需要扩展。

Method: 将认知成本重新定义为策略复杂度与加权条件熵项之和，其中条件熵项由新参数η控制。通过变分优化求解扩展后的目标函数，推导出最优策略仍然保持标准指数形式，但随着η增大策略变得更尖锐（决策更确定）。

Result: 扩展后的最优策略保留了标准指数形式，但允许策略精度（决策确定性）与奖励敏感度更独立地变化。这意味着标准政策压缩框架可能低估了动作选择的认知成本。

Conclusion: 该扩展能更好地解释人类决策中的偏差，但同时也增加了模型拟合人类数据的复杂度，这是未来工作需解决的问题。

Original Abstract: AI decision-support systems can benefit from anticipating biases in human decision-making. Many such biases may arise from human cognitive limitations. The policy compression framework models decision-making as a trade-off between reward maximization and the cognitive cost of encoding state-dependent action policies, formalized as the mutual information between states and actions (policy complexity). We argue that this account is incomplete because it treats conditional entropy--the irreducible uncertainty about which action should be selected given a state--as costless, even though empirical evidence suggests that it modulates reaction times. We therefore extend the framework by defining cognitive cost as the sum of policy complexity and a weighted conditional-entropy term, governed by a new parameter, $η$. The resulting optimal policy retains the standard exponential form but becomes sharper as $η$ increases, allowing policy precision to vary more independently of reward sensitivity. This modification implies that the standard policy compression framework may underestimate the cognitive cost of action selection, and it has the potential to better account for biases in human decision-making. At the same time, it introduces additional complexity for fitting the model to human data, which future work will need to address.

中文摘要翻译: AI决策支持系统可以从预判人类决策偏差中获益。许多此类偏差源于人类的认知局限性。政策压缩框架将决策建模为奖励最大化与编码状态依赖行动策略的认知成本之间的权衡，其中认知成本形式化为状态与动作之间的互信息（策略复杂度）。我们认为这一解释是不完整的，因为它将条件熵——给定状态下应选择哪个动作的不可约不确定性——视为无成本的，尽管经验证据表明条件熵会调节反应时。因此，我们通过将认知成本定义为策略复杂度与加权条件熵项之和来扩展该框架，其中加权条件熵项由新参数η控制。由此产生的最优策略保留了标准指数形式，但随着η增大而变得更尖锐，从而使策略精度能够更独立于奖励敏感度而变化。这一修改意味着标准政策压缩框架可能低估了动作选择的认知成本，并且有望更好地解释人类决策中的偏差。同时，这也增加了将模型拟合到人类数据的额外复杂度，这将是未来工作需要解决的问题。

</details>
